### [37] [GORIO: GPU-Centered Remote I/O for Graph ANNS over NVMe-oF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04415)
*Gen Zhang,Wenhao Gu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GORIO，一个将GPU为中心的本地I/O扩展到远程存储的系统，专门针对基于图的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）在NVMe over Fabrics上的优化，通过让GPU主导查询状态和I/O决策，CPU仅作为传输代理，实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的ANNS在向量数据库和RAG服务中广泛应用，但大索引超出单GPU服务器内存。现有远程存储路径以CPU为中心，CPU负责I/O请求、传输和恢复决策，这与ANNS图遍历的特性不匹配——下一次数据访问在GPU图遍历过程中发现。

Method: GORIO包含两层设计：1) GPU直通远程I/O路径，将本地页缓存缺失转化为分阶段远程操作；2) ANNS专用调度机制，将图遍历与远程页面服务重叠。GPU负责查询演进、页面缺失生成、待处理查询状态和恢复决策，CPU仅作为NVMe-oF传输和完成代理。

Result: 在SIFT1M DiskANN风格图工作量下，基于RDMA NVMe-oF路径，GORIO比最先进的远程I/O参考路径快1.31倍，比直接远程页缓存路径快3.73倍。

Conclusion: GORIO证明了针对图ANNS的以GPU为中心的远程I/O子系统的可行性和有效性，展示了GPU主导I/O决策相比传统CPU中心路径的显著优势。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) is increasingly used in vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation services, but large vector indexes often exceed the memory capacity of a single GPU server. NVMe over Fabrics (NVMe-oF) provides an attractive storage-disaggregation substrate, yet existing remote storage paths are still largely CPU-centered: the CPU forms I/O requests, drives transport progress, and determines when GPU computation can resume. This organization is poorly matched to graph ANNS, where the next data access is discovered inside GPU graph traversal.
  This paper presents GORIO, a system study that extends GPU-centered local I/O to remote storage and specializes the resulting substrate for graph ANNS over NVMe-oF. GORIO keeps query evolution, page-miss generation, pending-query state, and resume decisions on the GPU, while the CPU acts only as an NVMe-oF transport and completion proxy. The design has two layers: a GPU-direct remote I/O path that turns local page-cache misses into split-phase remote operations, and ANNS-specific scheduling mechanisms that overlap graph traversal with remote page service. On a SIFT1M DiskANN-style graph workload over an RDMA NVMe-oF path, GORIO is 1.31$\times$ faster than the state-of-the-art remote-I/O reference path and 3.73$\times$ faster than the direct remote page-cache path. These results demonstrate a concrete GPU-centered remote I/O substrate for graph ANNS.

</details>


### [38] [Adaptive Space-efficient Collectives for Dynamic and Unstructured Sparsity on GPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04676)
*Lannie Dalton Hough,Emir Gencer,Hoffmann Muki,Abhinav Bhatele*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Pici格式和基于位向量的稀疏感知集合通信算法（all-gather, reduce-scatter, all-reduce），在99%稀疏度下相比NCCL实现最高5.25倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集合通信库（如NCCL）仅针对稠密数据优化，处理稀疏数据时因格式转换开销和归约操作中的稠密化问题导致效率低下。

Method: 设计基于位向量的轻量级稀疏格式Pici，支持低开销的压缩/解压；提出自适应稀疏度的集合通信算法，在通信过程中动态调整数据表示。

Result: 在99%输入稀疏度下，all-gather、reduce-scatter、all-reduce分别实现5.25倍、2.5倍和2.66倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 稀疏感知的集合通信原语能显著降低通信量，Pici格式在中等稀疏度下具有低开销优势，自适应策略有效避免了不必要的稠密化。

Abstract: High-performance collective communication primitives are necessary for a variety of high performance computing (HPC) and machine learning (ML) workloads. State-of-the-art collective communication libraries such as NCCL optimize exclusively for dense data. However, when sending sparse data, we can reduce communication volume by not sending zeros. Unfortunately, explicitly handling sparsity introduces challenges such as format conversion overheads and densification during collectives that involve reductions. In this paper, we introduce sparsity-exploiting algorithms for three collectives that address these challenges: all-gather, reduce-scatter, and all-reduce. Our collective implementations are backed by a new bitvector-based format, Pici, designed for low overhead and fast (de)compression at moderate sparsities. Further, our algorithms adapt to the level of sparsity in data, modifying its representation during the course of the collective. At 99% input sparsity, our collectives achieve up to 5.25x, 2.5x, and 2.66x speedups over NCCL for all-gather, reduce-scatter, and all-reduce, respectively.

</details>


### [39] [Direct Model State Migration for Elastic Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04749)
*Weijian Liu,Mingzhen Li,Rui Kang,Chen Sun,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ETC，一种无检查点的状态迁移框架，用于弹性混合并行LLM训练，通过直接点对点通信和通信合并减少迁移开销2.33-6.37倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检查点的状态迁移方案在动态资源场景下需要将完整模型状态持久化到存储再恢复，导致GPU停滞和高延迟，无法高效支持弹性训练。

Method: 利用状态局部性最小化GPU间数据移动，用直接点对点通信替代存储持久化；通过通信合并消除节点碎片化。集成到Megatron-LM中实现。

Result: 相比基于检查点的方案，在各种并行配置下迁移开销降低2.33倍到6.37倍。

Conclusion: ETC通过无检查点的状态迁移框架实现了高效的弹性训练，适合生产环境中的动态资源场景。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training shall adapt to dynamic resources in shared clusters to tackle the elasticity, including passive preemption and optimistic scaling. State migration across device sets is required when altering the hybrid-parallel configuration due to dynamic resources. Existing solutions rely on checkpoint-based mechanisms, which persist complete states to storage for resuming with re-assigned resources, forcing all GPUs to stall when transferring model states. Despite optimization efforts, checkpoint-based solutions incur prohibitive latency due to data movement across memory hierarchies.
  We propose ETC, a checkpoint-free state migration framework for elastic hybrid-parallel LLM training. We exploits the state locality to minimize inter-GPU data movement, replacing storage persistence with direct peer-to-peer communication. Besides, we eliminate node fragmentation through communication coalescing. Integrated with Megatron-LM, ETC reduces migration overhead by 2.33$\times$ to 6.37$\times$ compared to checkpoint-based solutions across diverse parallel configurations. By enabling efficient migration, ETC unlocks practical elastic training in production environments.

</details>


### [40] [When Words Predict Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04951)
*Anubhab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一个CPU端语言资源预测（LRF）网关，通过文本结构向量和XGBoost预测器，在边缘GPU内存分配前动态路由LLM请求，避免OOM崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分布式LLM调度器依赖静态token计数或滚动延迟平均值，在处理EPO专利权利要求等语言刚性文本时失效，导致多模型集成扩展引发KV缓存和权重分配激增，使消费级边缘GPU显存溢出崩溃。

Method: 1) 提取16维文本结构向量；2) XGBoost预测器判断是否属于陷阱带（trap-band）；3) 计算逃离概率P_esc；4) 通过实时延迟遥测计算动态闭合路由阈值T_route(t)；5) 在分配GPU内存前将请求路由到本地Qwen2.5-7B边缘worker或远程H100上的对比集成模型。

Result: 在6000请求现场试验中，误路由率R_mis降至0.087-0.095（token计数基线为0.849）；峰值边缘VRAM安全限制在4.82GiB（低于8GiB上限）且在27倍WAN延迟变化下保持稳定；AUROC达0.84；动态T_route(t)相比静态阈值减少8.2%误路由。

Conclusion: LRF网关有效解决了LLM推理中因语言刚性文本导致的调度失效和OOM崩溃问题，通过CPU侧前置预测和动态路由实现了边缘设备上的安全高效推理。

Abstract: Standard distributed \ac{llm} schedulers rely on static token counts or rolling latency averages, making them susceptible to failures on statutorily constrained text. On \ac{epo} claims governed by Article 84 \ac{epc}, linguistic rigidity makes human and machine authorship statistically indistinguishable. Resolving this ambiguity mid-flight forces dynamic multi-model ensemble expansion, triggering unpredictable KV-cache and weight-allocation spikes that saturate consumer-grade edge GPU VRAM and cause severe \ac{oom} crashes. To prevent hardware collapse, we propose a CPU-side Linguistic Resource Forecasting (LRF) gateway. The gateway extracts a 16-dimensional text-structure vector and applies an XGBoost predictor to forecast trap-band membership. The resulting escalation probability ($\Pesc$) is evaluated against a dynamic, closed-form routing threshold ($\Tauroute(t)$) computed via real-time latency telemetry. Requests are safely routed to either a local Qwen2.5-7B edge worker or a remote contrastive ensemble (Qwen2.5 7B + 32B) on an NVIDIA H100 \emph{before} any edge GPU memory is allocated. In a 6,000-request live trial, the LRF gateway reduced the operational misroute fraction ($R_{\mathrm{mis}}$) to $0.087$--$0.095$, an order of magnitude below the token-count baseline ($0.849$). Peak edge VRAM remained safely bounded at $\SI{4.82}{\gibi\byte}$ (under the $\SI{8}{\gibi\byte}$ ceiling) across a $27\times$ variation in \ac{wan} delay. The predictor achieved a live-trial AUROC of $0.84$, and the dynamic $\Tauroute(t)$ controller yielded an $8.2\%$ relative reduction in misroutes compared to an equivalent static threshold.

</details>


### [41] [Communication-Aware Placement and Pruning for Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05116)
*Xiao Shi,Yingying Sun,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CAP是一个面向MoE模型分布式推理的通信感知分配与剪枝框架，通过协同激活驱动的专家放置、通信-计算权衡调整和通信感知专家剪枝，在不同硬件配置下实现1.23x-1.86x的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MoE模型扩展至数百个专家，放置和剪枝决策显著影响通信量，进而影响跨GPU和节点的分布式推理性能，现有方法未同时考虑计算、通信和精度三者。

Method: CAP包含三个组件：(1) 协同激活驱动的专家放置，将频繁共同激活的专家分组以减少通信；(2) 通信-计算权衡调整，生成不同计算负载和通信量的放置方案；(3) 通信感知专家剪枝，选择性移除路由目标以减少通信同时控制精度损失。

Result: 在单节点和多节点实验中，相比DeepSeek EPLB和vLLM中的顺序放置，CAP实现了1.23x-1.86x的吞吐量提升，并在有损加速下保持更好的模型精度。

Conclusion: CAP通过联合优化专家放置与剪枝，为不同硬件配置提供高效的MoE推理策略，在提升吞吐量的同时较好地保持了模型精度。

Abstract: As MoE models scale to hundreds of experts, placement and pruning decisions increasingly dictate communication volume, affecting the performance of distributed inference across GPUs and nodes. We propose CAP (Communication-Aware Assignment and Pruning), a framework that considers computation, communication and accuracy together for efficient MoE inference through expert placement and pruning. It consists of three components: (1) Co-activation driven expert placement, which groups frequently co-activated experts to reduce inter-device and inter-node communication; (2) Communicationcomputation trade-off adjustment, which generates placements with different computational load and communication volume; and (3) Communication-aware expert pruning, which selectively removes routing destinations to reduce communication with limited accuracy degradation. By combining these components, CAP selects an efficient operating strategy for different hardware configurations. Across our single-node and multi-node experiments, it achieves 1.23x - 1.86 x throughput improvement over DeepSeek EPLB and sequential placement in vLLM, and preserves better model accuracy at the same target speedup under lossy acceleration.

</details>

### [42] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: A Validity Audit of Tool-Calling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02577)
*Vishvesh Bhat,Jay Vaghasiya,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Asad Aali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对四大工具调用基准家族（BFCL v4、τ2-Bench、LiveMCPBench、MCP-Atlas）进行了系统性的有效性和可重复性审计，发现18.5%的评估者-人工不一致率，揭示了确定性基准和LLM评判基准中的多种系统性失败模式，并提出了统一的失败分类法、修正后的评估组件以及Tool-Veritas和Harness Lab等工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具调用基准分数常被视为真实结果，但评估者本身的有效性和可靠性未经检验，这可能导致排名反映的是评估者的伪影而非真实能力。

Method: 对四个主流工具调用基准家族进行系统性审计，通过496个专家审查的基准任务，分析评估者与人类判断的一致性，并系统分类失败模式。

Result: 发现92处评估者-人工不一致（18.5%错误率），确定性基准存在状态匹配脆弱、轨迹锁定、错误真实答案、子串通信失败和奖励基础不对齐等问题；LLM评判基准存在评分标准漂移、幻觉完成、仅答案评分及大幅运行间方差（如LiveMCPBench同一设置下分数从57.9%到76.8%）。

Conclusion: 当前工具调用分数可能反映评估者伪影而非智能体真实能力，需要可复现、可审计且与人类判断对齐的评估者；提出了分解式指标并开源了Tool-Veritas和Harness Lab。

Abstract: Tool-calling benchmarks are increasingly used to rank language-model agents, yet their scores are often treated as ground truth without validating the evaluators themselves. We present a systematic validity and reproducibility audit of four major tool-calling benchmark families: BFCL v4, τ2-Bench, LiveMCPBench, and MCP-Atlas. Across 496 expert-reviewed benchmark tasks, we find 92 evaluator-human disagreements, corresponding to an 18.5% misalignment rate. The failures are not isolated annotation mistakes: deterministic benchmarks exhibit brittle state matching, trajectory lock-in, incorrect ground truths, substring-based communication failures, and reward-basis misalignment, while LLM-judge benchmarks exhibit rubric drift, hallucinated completion, answer-only scoring, and substantial run-to-run variance. In LiveMCPBench, 23 repeated evaluations of the same setup produce scores ranging from 57.9% to 76.8%, a spread of 18.9 percentage points, large enough to change leaderboard conclusions. These results show that current tool-calling scores can reflect evaluator artifacts rather than agent capability. We introduce a unified taxonomy of tool-calling evaluation failures, release trace-level audit artifacts and corrected evaluation components, and argue for decomposed metrics that separately measure tool invocation, task completion, and outcome verification. Our findings suggest that progress in tool-using agents requires benchmarks whose evaluators are themselves reproducible, auditable, and aligned with human judgments of task success. We further introduce Tool-Veritas, a configurable benchmark that combines deterministic state verification with optional qualitative judging, and Harness Lab, an open-source system for benchmark execution, trace inspection, repeated-run comparison, and evaluator debugging.

</details>


### [43] [Knowledge-Centric Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02609)
*Mariano Garralda-Barrio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"知识架构"这一新学科，将经典数据工程原则（ETL、CDC、血缘、目录等）重新定义以适用于知识制品时代，认为企业AI系统正推动组织知识从被动信息资源转变为可操作的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的兴起暴露了传统数据工程在处理组织知识时的局限性，知识不再是被动资源而是可执行的基础设施，需要新的架构原则来管理。

Method: 提出概念模型和分类法，将数据工程中的核心概念映射到知识工程领域（如ETL→知识摄入、CDC→知识变更检测、血缘→来源追溯等），并分析LLM Wiki和Open Knowledge Format作为早期证据。

Result: 建立了从数据工程到知识架构的完整映射框架，定义了知识制品的生命周期管理原则。

Conclusion: 组织知识应被视为可操作资产，知识架构是支撑企业AI系统的基础性学科，而非单纯的LLM应用问题。

Abstract: For decades, data engineering has developed mature architectural principles for integrating, governing, validating, cataloging, and serving organizational data. The rise of large language models does not eliminate these concerns; it exposes a broader version of them. Organizational knowledge is becoming executable infrastructure: systems increasingly retrieve it, assemble it, reason over it, and act on it. This paper argues that enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) systems suggest a transition toward an architectural discipline for representing, maintaining, governing, and operationally delivering organizational knowledge. We refer to this discipline as \emph{knowledge architecture}. We offer a conceptual model and taxonomy showing how classical data-engineering guarantees must be redefined when the managed unit shifts from records to knowledge artifacts: extract, transform, and load (ETL) becomes knowledge ingestion, change-data capture (CDC) becomes knowledge change detection, lineage becomes provenance, catalogs become knowledge catalogs, materialized views become knowledge views, and medallion architectures become raw--curated--operational knowledge layers. Emerging formats such as large language model (LLM) Wiki and the Open Knowledge Format (OKF) are treated as early evidence of this transition, not as its endpoint. The central claim is that knowledge architecture becomes useful when organizational knowledge ceases to be a passive information resource and becomes an operational asset used by humans, agents, workflows, and models to execute work.

</details>


### [44] [Dependencies that Bundle Code and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02618)
*Aman Sharma,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LIBCACHE是首个将每个依赖的执行缓存作为一等工件通过包注册表（Maven Central）分发的系统。每个库维护者从自身测试套件生成执行缓存并发布，构建时合并所有依赖的缓存以加速应用启动。在三个Java项目上评估，启动速度提升最高1.91倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用依赖只包含编译代码，不包含执行状态，每次启动都需要从头重建状态。现有执行缓存系统无法将缓存作为可分发工件发布到包注册表供下游复用。

Method: 库维护者从测试套件生成执行缓存并发布到Maven Central；构建时LIBCACHE合并所有依赖的缓存为统一缓存，覆盖各依赖测试套件中涉及的类。

Result: 在三个真实Java项目的12个工作负载上，启动速度提升最高1.91倍；10/12工作负载优于单工作负载缓存；batik平均提升1.60x vs 1.27x；缓存生产增加10-47%构建时间；缓存大小平均为对应JAR的18倍。

Conclusion: 执行缓存是可分发的供应链构件，合并每个依赖的缓存比单次观察运行提供更广泛的启动覆盖。

Abstract: Applications are built from many dependencies. Each dependency is distributed by package registries but it only contains compiled code and does not ship execution state, so runtimes rebuild that state from scratch on every startup. Execution caching records this preparation result so subsequent starts can skip it, but no existing system publishes execution state to a registry for downstream consumers to reuse. We introduce LIBCACHE, the first system to distribute per-dependency execution caches through a package registry as first-class artifacts alongside compiled code. Each library maintainer produces an execution cache from the library's own test suite and publishes it to Maven Central alongside the JAR. At application build time, LIBCACHE merges all per-dependency caches into a single unified cache that covers the classes exercised across every dependency's test suite. We evaluate LIBCACHE on three real-world Java projects across twelve workloads. LIBCACHE speeds up application startup by up to 1.91x and outperforms a cache built from a single workload in 10 of 12 workloads, with the largest average gain for batik (1.60x vs. 1.27x). Cache production adds 10-47% to build time for applications, and execution caches average 18x the size of the corresponding JAR. Our main contribution is LIBCACHE, which establishes that execution caches are distributable supply chain artifacts and that merging per-dependency caches delivers broader startup coverage than any single observation run.

</details>


### [45] [Can Coding Agents Implement Missed Compiler Optimizations? Evaluating LLM Agents on LLVM Peephole Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02684)
*Hongxu Xu,Chunhao Liao,Xintong Zhou,Chengnian Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 PeepholeBench，一个基于 LLVM InstCombine pass 中真实错过的窥孔优化问题构建的评测框架，用于评估代码智能体在编译器优化开发方面的能力。实验表明，现有代码智能体在正确性和收益性上均无法达到人类开发者的水平，主要失败模式包括过度狭窄的转换和误用 LLVM 特定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于大语言模型的代码智能体已能够修补实际代码库，但它们能否开发编译器优化尚不清楚。为系统研究这一问题，需要构建一个真实、有挑战性的评测基准。

Method: 从 LLVM 的 InstCombine pass 中收集 21 个已解决的 issue 和 19 个合并的 PR，构建 PeepholeBench 评测框架，仅提供修复前的上下文信息，评估代码智能体生成的补丁的正确性和收益性。

Result: 现有最先进的代码智能体在正确性和收益性两个维度上均无法与人类开发者匹敌，存在过度狭窄的转换和 LLVM 特定机制误用等主要失败模式。

Conclusion: PeepholeBench 为代码智能体在编译器优化开发领域提供了真实且具有挑战性的基准，未来需要构建更可靠的智能体来辅助编译器优化开发。

Abstract: Coding agents built on large language models are now capable of patching sizable real-world codebases, yet whether they can develop compiler optimizations remains an open question. To study this question, we introduce PeepholeBench, an evaluation framework whose tasks are constructed from real-world missed peephole optimizations reported against LLVM's InstCombine pass. Since missed peephole optimizations are typically fixed with small, localized patches, they offer a well-scoped but demanding testbed for coding agents: a correct fix demands rigorous reasoning about program semantics along with familiarity with optimizer-specific conventions. PeepholeBench derives its tasks from 21 resolved LLVM issues and 19 merged pull requests (PRs), supplies agents with only the issue context that existed before each fix, and assesses the resulting patches for both correctness and profitability.
  With PeepholeBench, we benchmark state-of-the-art coding agents on fixing missed peephole optimizations in LLVM's InstCombine pass, measuring their patches against the corresponding human-written fixes. We observe a tension between correctness and profitability, and no agent matches human developers on both dimensions at once. The dominant failure modes are overly narrow transformations and misuse of LLVM-specific mechanisms, errors that existing test suites rarely expose. Together, these results establish PeepholeBench as a realistic and challenging benchmark for coding agents, and suggest future directions for building agents that can more dependably assist compiler optimization development.

</details>


### [46] [LLMoxie: Exploring Agentic AI for Scientific Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02703)
*Landung Setiawan,Anant Mittal,Cordero Core,Anshul Tambay,Carlos Garcia Jurado Suarez,David A. C. Beck,Andrew J. Connolly,Vani Mandava*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMoxie是一个三层架构的AI平台，支持多云和本地推理，结合LiteLLM/MLflow控制平面实现认证、预算、PII掩码和可观测性，以及AI编码代理的应用增强层。其上构建的开源RSE-Plugins生态系统将RSE知识编码为插件-代理-技能层次结构，覆盖科学Python实践、领域知识、六阶段研发流程和项目生命周期管理。文章报告了在大学RSE中心20个月的实践经验，展示了如何将AI编码代理从通用代码生成器转变为尊重领域规范、产生可审计推理轨迹的领域感知协作者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI编码代理针对商业软件基准优化，不适用于科研软件环境：它们忽略科学Python库的调用约定、误处理敏感或受限数据、留下难以事后重建的决策轨迹。科研软件更看重可引用、可审计、可复现和可扩展性，而非原始代码质量，需要专门的基础设施和治理方案来弥合这一差距。

Method: 设计了三层架构的LLMoxie平台：支持多云和本地推理的底层、LiteLLM/MLflow控制平面（认证、预算、PII掩码、可观测性）、AI编码代理应用增强层。上层构建开源RSE-Plugins生态系统，通过插件-代理-技能层次结构编码积累的RSE知识。在跨天文学、地球气候科学、农业和健康项目的大学RSE中心进行了20个月的实践验证。

Result: 总结了多领域RSE中心采用Agentic AI时面临的基础设施、治理和流程挑战，描述了平台和插件设计，并从实际科学软件部署中提炼了运营经验。平台和插件将AI编码代理转变为领域感知的协作者，能够尊重社区规范并产生可审计的技术推理溯源。

Conclusion: 通过LLMoxie平台和RSE-Plugins生态系统，AI编码代理可以从通用代码生成器转变为领域感知协作者，在科研软件场景下提供可引用、可审计、可复现的代码生成能力，同时尊重科学社区规范和数据处理要求。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe LLMoxie, an institutional AI platform whose three-tiered architecture supports multi-cloud and on-premise inference, a LiteLLM/MLflow control plane for authentication, budgeting, PII masking, and observability, and an application augmentation layer for AI coding agents. Layered on top, an open-source RSE-Plugins ecosystem encodes accumulated RSE knowledge as a Plugin-Agent-Skill hierarchy spanning scientific Python practice, domain-specific knowledge, a six-phase research-and-implement workflow, and project lifecycle management. Scientific software is judged less by raw code quality than by whether it can be cited, audited, reproduced, and extended. Off-the-shelf AI coding agents, optimized against commercial software benchmarks, are poorly calibrated for this setting: they ignore the conventions of the scientific Python libraries they invoke, mishandle sensitive or embargoed data, and leave decision trails that are difficult to reconstruct after the fact. We report on twenty months of practice at a university-based research software engineering (RSE) center, where RSEs embedded across astronomy, earth and climate science, agriculture, and health projects worked to close this gap. We characterize the recurring infrastructure, governance, and process challenges of adopting Agentic AI inside a multi-domain RSE center, describe the platform and plugin design, and distill operational lessons from real scientific software deployments. Together, the platform and plugins shift AI coding agents from generic code generators into domain-aware collaborators that respect community norms and produce auditable provenance of technical reasoning.

</details>


### [47] [SMOCS: A Streaming Framework for Simplified Deployment, Monitoring, and Optimization of ML Systems in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02731)
*Armen Kasparian,Kishansingh Rajput,Malachi Schram,John Vennekate*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SMOCS是一个基于Kafka和Docker的容器化框架，用于简化科学设施中ML模型的部署与维护，支持实时监控、优化和控制，具备在线持续学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学设施中部署和维护ML模型面临巨大工程挑战，每个设施都有独特的数据协议、非标准格式和基础设施约束，导致团队需要为每个新应用重建集成管道。

Method: 提出SMOCS框架，包含三层贡献：1) 基于Apache Kafka的分层抽象，将基础设施与应用程序逻辑分离；2) 三线程代理架构，在时间上解耦数据摄取、模型训练和实时推理，支持持续在线学习；3) 配置驱动部署模型，使领域专家无需软件工程专业知识即可操作ML管道。框架通过Docker容器化实现水平扩展和故障隔离。

Result: SMOCS是一个设施平台无关、故障隔离设计、可水平扩展的开源框架，已在Jefferson Lab GitHub上公开发布。

Conclusion: SMOCS通过Kafka和容器化技术有效解决了科学设施中ML部署的工程挑战，降低了领域专家使用ML管道的门槛。

Abstract: Machine learning has demonstrated significant potential for real-time monitoring, optimization, and control of scientific facilities. However, deploying and maintaining ML models in operational environments remains a substantial engineering challenge. Each facility presents unique data protocols, non-standard formats, and infrastructure constraints, forcing teams to rebuild integration pipelines for every new application. We present SMOCS (Streaming Monitoring Optimization and Control System), a Kafka-based containerized framework that addresses this challenge through three contributions: 1) a layered abstraction over Apache Kafka that separates infrastructure from application logic, 2) a three-thread agent architecture that temporally decouples data ingestion, model training, and real-time inference enabling continuous online learning from live data streams, and 3) a configuration-driven deployment model that enables domain experts to operate ML pipelines without software engineering expertise. SMOCS is facility platform-agnostic, fault-isolated by design, and horizontally scalable through Docker containerization. The framework is publicly available as open-source software on the Jefferson Lab Github.

</details>


### [48] [EvoOtter: Evolutionary Reproduction Test Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02854)
*Toufique Ahmed,Jatin Ganhotra,Avraham Shinnar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EvoOtter是一种结合进化编程与LLM的bug复现测试生成方法，通过逐次减半控制执行成本、批量交叉和规则突变降低LLM调用开销，以较低成本生成高质量BRT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理放缩的BRT生成方法成本高昂且反馈不可靠，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 采用进化编程框架，使用逐次减半（successive halving）控制测试执行成本，单次LLM调用进行整代批量交叉（batched crossover），结合基于规则的代码突变和专为BRT设计的适应度评分。

Result: EvoOtter以远低于先前推理放缩方法的成本，生成了最先进的BRT质量。

Conclusion: 展示了如何高效地将进化编程与LLM结合用于软件工程任务。

Abstract: Before fixing an issue, it is useful to first reproduce it by generating a bug reproduction test (BRT). However, generating a BRT is itself a challenging task, because issue descriptions tend to be informal, making it difficult to determine whether a candidate BRT indeed fails for the reason in the issue. Prior work has attempted to tackle this problem via inference scaling, using large language models to generate many BRTs and patches, then using execution feedback to select and improve them. Unfortunately, this is expensive and the feedback is unreliable. This paper explores evolutionary programming for BRT generation to sharpen the feedback, while enhancing evolutionary programming to keep costs in check. Our new approach, EvoOtter, controls test execution costs via successive halving. Furthermore, it controls LLM costs via batched crossover for an entire generation in a single LLM call, as well as via rule-based code mutations, with a new fitness score tailored for BRTs. As a result, EvoOtter generates state-of-the-art quality BRTs at the fraction of the cost of prior inference-scaling approaches to this problem. More broadly, this paper points at how to efficiently and effectively combine evolutionary programming with large language models for software engineering.

</details>


### [49] [Diagnosis-Driven Automatic Repair for Agentic Workflow via Symbolic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02882)
*Xuyan Ma,Yawen Wang,Junjie Wang,Xiaofei Xie,Boyu Wu,Mingyang Li,Dandan Wang,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FlowFixer是一个面向LLM驱动的agentic工作流的诊断驱动自动修复框架，通过符号化轨迹转换、符号推理推导可执行行为规范、失败归因与根因分析，生成针对性修复补丁，并采用多维度预执行评估过滤不可行修复。在Dify、Coze和n8n平台上，修复成功率达71.3%，比基线提升11.9%-27.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic工作流优化方法主要依赖轨迹级反馈，未能显式识别底层失败根因，导致修复方案缺乏针对性。亟需一种能够精确诊断失败根因并生成精确修复方案的方法。

Method: FlowFixer将工作流执行转换为统一符号轨迹，通过符号推理推导可执行行为规范（捕获节点正确性、时序依赖和因果关系），基于规范验证进行失败归因和根因分析，生成针对性修复补丁，并通过多维度预执行评估降低验证成本。

Result: 在Dify、Coze和n8n平台上，FlowFixer修复成功率达71.3%，优于基线11.9%-27.6%；失败归因准确率提升4.8%-33.1%，根因分析准确率提升15.3%-38.8%。

Conclusion: FlowFixer通过符号建模与推理为agentic工作流的可靠诊断与修复提供了新视角，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Platform-orchestrated agentic workflows have become a popular paradigm for developing LLM-based applications. However, their reliability remains a major challenge due to the uncertainty of LLM outputs, complex inter-node dependencies, and heterogeneous tool interactions. Existing agentic workflow optimization and agent enhancement methods primarily rely on trajectory-level feedback. Without explicitly identifying the underlying failure root causes, their resulting repair plans are often insufficiently targeted. We propose FlowFixer, a diagnosis-driven automated repair framework for agentic workflows. FlowFixer first transforms workflow executions into unified symbolic traces and performs symbolic inference to derive executable behavioral specifications that capture node correctness, temporal dependencies, and causal relationships. Based on specification verification, it conducts failure attribution and root cause analysis, and then generates targeted repair patches. To reduce verification costs, FlowFixer further employs a multi-dimensional pre-execution assessment to filter infeasible repairs before dynamic verification. We evaluate FlowFixer on workflow failures collected from three popular development platforms: Dify, Coze and n8n. Results show that FlowFixer achieves a repair success rate of 71.3%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by 11.9% to 27.6%. It also improves failure attribution accuracy by 4.8% to 33.1% and root cause analysis accuracy by 15.3% to 38.8%. This work offers a new perspective on reliable diagnosis and repair of agentic workflows through symbolic modeling and inference.

</details>


### [50] [CoACT: Action-Preserving Observation Compression for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02911)
*Haorui Chen,Yuancheng Zhu,Yitong Zhang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoACT是一种面向LLM编码智能体的动作保留型观察压缩方法，通过"下一动作保留"（NAP）机制确保压缩后的观察不会改变智能体的后续行为，在SWE-bench上实现33%的token节省同时保持任务解决效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体在与开发环境交互时，返回的观察结果不断积累到上下文中，导致推理成本大幅上升。现有压缩方法效率-效果权衡不佳，因为它们没有显式建模压缩如何影响智能体的后续行为。

Method: CoACT基于下一动作保留（NAP）原则：压缩后的观察需诱导出与原始观察相同的下一动作。训练时，教师模型为每个观察生成多个压缩候选，使用基于NAP的动作保留奖励过滤会改变智能体下一动作的候选，再使用长度缩减奖励选择紧凑候选作为轻量级压缩器的监督信号。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上使用三种智能体模型进行实验，CoACT将平均总token消耗降低33.0%，同时保持任务解决效果接近未压缩的智能体。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模压缩对智能体后续行为的影响，CoACT实现了更好的效率-效果权衡，为编码智能体的成本优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents solve software-engineering tasks through iterative interactions with development environments, where returned observations accumulate in the context and become a major source of inference cost. Observation compression reduces this cost by shortening observations before they are appended to the context. However, existing methods still exhibit an unsatisfactory efficiency-effectiveness trade-off, as they do not explicitly model how compression affects the agent's subsequent behavior. This paper proposes CoACT, an action-preserving observation compression method for coding agents. CoACT is built on next-action preservation (NAP), which requires a compressed observation to induce the same next action as the raw observation. By checking the agent's immediate next action, NAP provides a practical signal for whether a compression preserves the information needed for continued task solving. During training, a teacher model first generates multiple compressed candidates of each observation. CoACT then uses an action-preservation reward based on NAP to filter out candidates that would change the agent's next action, and uses a length-reduction reward to choose compact candidates as supervision for a lightweight compressor. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified with three agentic models show that CoACT reduces average total token consumption by 33.0% while maintaining task-solving effectiveness close to the uncompressed agent.

</details>


### [51] [Can LLMs Really Recover Microservice Failures? A Recovery-Aware Evaluation of Diagnosis-to-Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04623)
*Jiaxing Qi,Zhongzhi Luan,Hongyu Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Carol Fung,Yongxin Tong,Hailong Yang,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: R2Act 是一个针对云原生微服务系统中故障恢复动作评估的框架与基准数据集，包含302个Kubernete incidents，用于评估从诊断到恢复动作的转化能力。实验表明即使RCA准确率高（91.4%-99.7%），恢复动作有效性仍很低（36.8%-60.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA和日志分析评估方法只关注诊断阶段，缺乏对诊断后恢复动作决策环节的评估，而恢复需要将诊断转化为具体的恢复动作并验证服务健康恢复。

Method: 定义了incident schema、quality gate、action-space表示、恢复有效性指标、离线评估器和在线重放协议，构建了302个Kubernete incidents基准数据集，评估启发式、监督、RCA导向、深度日志和LLM方法。

Result: 最强RAG-based LLM的根因服务准确率达91.4%-99.7%，但恢复有效性仅36.8%-60.3%；即使根因服务和故障类型都正确，仍有39.5%-62.0%选择无效恢复动作。

Conclusion: 恢复失败主要源于将诊断证据转化为有效恢复动作和可接受目标的困难，而非缺乏诊断知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to interpret operational evidence and assist incident response in cloud-native microservice systems. However, recovery-oriented use cases require more than identifying a root cause. After observing symptoms and diagnosing a fault, an operator or agent must translate the diagnosis into a concrete recovery action, apply it to an admissible target, and verify that service health has been restored. Existing RCA and log-analysis evaluations are well-suited to diagnosis, but they do not characterize this subsequent action decision. This paper presents R2Act, a recovery-action evaluation framework for post-diagnosis incident response. R2Act defines an incident schema, quality gate, action-space representation, recovery-validity metrics, offline evaluator, and live-replay protocol. We instantiate the framework as a benchmark dataset of 302 quality-audited Kubernetes incidents from \system. Each incident provides synchronized multi-modal observations, root-cause labels, an incident-specific action space, and annotated valid and invalid recovery plans. We evaluate heuristic, supervised, RCA-oriented, deep log, and LLM-based methods. The strongest RAG-based LLMs reach 91.4\%--99.7\% root-cause service accuracy, yet their recovery validity remains only 36.8\%--60.3\%. Even when both the root-cause service and fault type are correct, recovery-oriented methods still choose invalid actions for 39.5\%--62.0\% of correctly diagnosed incidents. Overall, this work reveals that many recovery failures arise not from missing diagnostic knowledge, but from the difficulty of translating diagnostic evidence into valid recovery actions and admissible targets. This work provides a reproducible, simplified starting point for research and evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [SkillOpt-Lite: Better and Faster Agent Self-evolution via One Line of Vibe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03451)
*Yifei Shen,Bo Li,Xinjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillOpt-Lite，一个基于零阶优化的最小可行技能优化管线，通过文件系统级轨迹探索、共识属性挖掘和独立验证门控三个原则实现收敛与泛化，在多个基准上超越完整版SkillOpt，并展示了从技能优化到完整工具链优化（HarnessOpt）的自然泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体技能优化方法依赖复杂管线，缺乏对"最小可行管线"的探讨。本文旨在形式化技能优化问题，找到每个组件都有理论或实证依据的最小管线。

Method: 将技能优化形式化为零阶优化问题，建立了三个原则：文件系统级轨迹探索、共识属性挖掘、独立验证门控。去除冗余后提出SkillOpt-Lite，并将所有智能体组件视为标准可编辑代码，自然泛化到完整工具链优化（HarnessOpt）。

Result: SkillOpt-Lite在LiveMath上比完整SkillOpt提升GPT-5.5 +8.8分、GPT-5.4-nano +25.4分，nano模型超越标准GPT-5.4。HarnessOpt在SpreadsheetBench上使GPT-5.4-nano达0.7758准确率，超过更大模型GPT-5.5的0.7620。

Conclusion: 最小化管线不仅可行而且在多个场景中超越复杂方案，证明了"少即是多"的设计哲学。该方法已集成到VSCode Copilot等生产级编码智能体中。

Abstract: While skill optimization for autonomous agents has gained traction, existing methods rely on complex pipelines. This leaves a fundamental question unaddressed: What constitutes a minimal viable pipeline for skill optimization, where every component is justified by theory or empirical necessity? We formalize skill optimization via Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization, mapping classical counterparts (central difference, trust regions) to recent literature. Noting that unlike blind numerical perturbations in classical ZO, skill trajectories serve as interpretable debugging feedback. Grounded in Claude Code philosophy and PAC learning, we establish three principles for convergence and generalization: file-system-based trajectory exploration, consensus attribute mining, and independent validation gating. Eliminating redundancies, we propose SkillOpt-Lite. It accelerates convergence and outperforms full SkillOpt: improving LiveMath by +8.8 points on GPT-5.5 and +25.4 points on GPT-5.4-nano, allowing the nano model to surpass standard GPT-5.4 optimized by SkillOpt. Finally, we integrate our framework into production coding agents like VSCode Copilot, enabling developers to evolve agent skills via one line of vibe. Because our framework treats all agent components simply as standard editable code, this minimal pipeline naturally generalizes to full harness optimization (HarnessOpt). On SpreadsheetBench, HarnessOpt enables GPT-5.4-nano to achieve 0.7758 accuracy, outperforming the larger GPT-5.5 running standard pipelines (0.7620). Code is available at https://github.com/EvolvingLMMs-Lab/SkillOpt-Lite.

</details>


### [53] [CAGE-1: Control, Assurance, and Governance Evaluation for Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03510)
*Roopam W. Sure*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CAGE-1 框架，用于评估企业级 AI Agent 在部署前的控制、保证和治理能力，涵盖权限、策略、检索质量、内存完整性、工具安全、可审计性、人工监督等 12 个维度，并引入 Prebind Assurance 概念确保代理行为在产生业务影响前可被管控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI 正从实验转向运营，Agent 系统开始执行规划、记忆、工具调用等复杂操作，传统仅评估答案准确性和流畅度的方式已无法满足治理需求，需要新的评估框架来回答谁授权操作、是否合规、证据是否最新、工具调用是否被允许等治理问题。

Method: 提出 CAGE-1 (Control, Assurance, and Governance Evaluation for Enterprise Agentic AI) 评估框架，从权威性、策略执行、检索质量、内存完整性、工具安全、可审计性、人工监督、冲突处理、安全失败、Prebind Assurance、运营就绪度和业务适配度 12 个维度进行系统化评估，其中 Prebind Assurance 用于在代理行为产生约束性后果前验证其是否可控。

Result: CAGE-1 框架定义了代理行为在成为具有业务影响的约束性操作前，可被判定为允许、暂缓、缩小、拒绝、升级、隔离或无效化，从而实现对 Agent 行为的事前控制评估。

Conclusion: CAGE-1 为企业部署 AI Agent 提供了系统化的就绪度评估框架，填补了传统仅关注准确性和流畅度的评估空白，将治理和管控能力纳入评判标准。

Abstract: Enterprise artificial intelligence is moving from experimentation into operational workflows. Early programs focused on model access and retrieval-augmented generation, but enterprises are now beginning to deploy agents that plan, retrieve, remember, call tools, update systems, and coordinate work across applications.
  This changes the evaluation problem. Leaders are no longer asking only whether an answer is accurate or fluent. They need to know who authorized an action, which policy applied, whether evidence was current, whether memory was valid, whether a tool call was permitted, whether the decision can be replayed, and whether the agent can be stopped before it creates business impact.
  This paper introduces CAGE-1: Control, Assurance, and Governance Evaluation for Enterprise Agentic AI. CAGE-1 is an evaluation framework for deciding whether enterprise agents are ready for deployment. It evaluates authority, policy enforcement, retrieval quality, memory integrity, tool safety, auditability, human oversight, conflict handling, safe failure, Prebind Assurance, operational readiness, and business fitness.
  CAGE-1 introduces Prebind Assurance to describe the evaluated ability to prove that an agentic action is controlled before it becomes binding, effective, or operationally consequential. The framework tests whether a proposed action is admitted, held, narrowed, refused, escalated, quarantined, or made non-effective before protected consequence forms.

</details>


### [54] [AGL-1: The Enterprise AI Governance Layer as a Control Plane for Trusted Enterprise Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03516)
*Roopam W. Sure*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AGL-1，一个面向企业AI的治理层参考模型，涵盖身份感知检索、策略执行、溯源管理、记忆治理、知识完整性监控、智能体执行控制和信任可观测性七个领域，旨在解决企业AI从实验走向生产运营时面临的治理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI正在从孤立实验转向运营依赖，核心挑战已不再是模型访问或推理规模，而是如何在分布式AI资产中执行授权、维护上下文溯源、控制持久记忆、检测过时/冲突知识、约束智能体执行并生成可审计证据。

Method: 基于GKS-5的知识治理原则进行泛化，从检索特定控制扩展到全AI执行路径治理，识别七类失效模式并对应定义七个治理领域，形成供应商中立的参考模型。

Result: 定义了AGL-1模型，涵盖身份感知检索、策略执行、溯源管理、记忆治理、知识完整性监控、智能体执行控制和信任可观测性七个治理域。

Conclusion: 企业AI的持久价值将越来越依赖于规模化治理能力，信任不是模型的属性，而是模型周围整个系统（身份、知识、策略、记忆、工具、人类监督和证据）协同工作的结果。

Abstract: Enterprise artificial intelligence is moving from isolated experimentation toward operational dependency across copilots, retrieval-augmented generation systems, autonomous agents, and AI-enabled business workflows. As this transition accelerates, the primary enterprise challenge is no longer only model access or inference scale. It is governed intelligence operations: the ability to enforce authorization, preserve contextual lineage, control persistent memory, detect stale or conflicting knowledge, constrain agentic execution, and produce audit-ready evidence across distributed AI estates.
  This paper introduces AGL-1, the Enterprise AI Governance Layer, as a vendor-neutral reference model for the control plane that should operate across foundation models, retrieval systems, orchestration frameworks, enterprise memory, policy engines, observability systems, tools, APIs, and business applications.
  Building on governed knowledge-system principles introduced in GKS-5, AGL-1 generalizes the governance problem from retrieval-specific controls to full AI execution-path governance. It identifies recurring failure modes such as unauthorized retrieval, stale grounding, unmanaged memory, weak provenance, policy drift, fragmented observability, and uncontrolled autonomous execution. It then defines seven governance domains: identity-aware retrieval, policy enforcement, provenance management, memory governance, knowledge integrity monitoring, agentic execution control, and trust observability.
  The central claim is that durable enterprise value from AI will increasingly depend on the ability to govern intelligence at scale. In complex enterprises, trust is not a property of the model alone. It is a property of the system around the model: identity, knowledge, policy, memory, tools, human oversight, and evidence working together as a managed control plane.

</details>


### [55] [GameEngineBench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Real C++ Runtime Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03525)
*Brian La,Sejoon Chang,Ben Kim,Junyoung Bae,Aamish Ahmad Beg,Sei Chang,Gonzalo Gonzalez-Pumariega*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 GameEngineBench，一个基于 Unreal Engine 5 的基准测试，用于评估编码代理在实时交互式 C++ 开发中的能力，涵盖 110 个来自真实游戏仓库的任务。最强模型 pass@1 仅为 55.5%，31 个任务所有配置均无法解决，表明前沿编码代理在深度集成的 C++ 实时系统开发上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏引擎在医疗、机器人、建筑、制造等领域有广泛应用，但其 C++ 开发复杂性对编码代理构成挑战。现有软件工程评估缺乏对实时交互系统的覆盖，因此需要专门基准来评估编码代理在状态化、交互式、实时系统（如 Unreal Engine）中修改 C++ 代码的能力。

Method: 从九个真实游戏仓库构建 110 个任务，涵盖游戏机制、多人行为、AI 与场景编排、动画与运动、UI、加载行为、在线服务集成、数据序列化、XR 行为、渲染插件等类别。要求模型在可执行 Unreal Engine 项目中进行原生 C++ 修改，确保编译通过并通过行为测试。

Result: 在 12 种配置中，最强模型达到 55.5% pass@1，31 个任务所有配置均无法解决。

Conclusion: 前沿编码代理在深度集成的 C++ 实时交互软件开发中仍存在显著困难，游戏引擎基准是现有软件工程评估的重要补充。

Abstract: Game engines provide real-time simulation, rendering, physics, interaction, networking, and asset pipelines, making them valuable not only for games but also for 3D applications in healthcare, robotics, architecture, manufacturing, and related domains. Because game development is where these systems are most mature and publicly available, it offers a practical testbed for evaluating coding agents that must modify C++ code within stateful, interactive, real-time systems. We present GameEngineBench, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on scoped C++ implementation tasks inside Unreal Engine 5 projects, built from nine real-world game repositories. The evaluation set consists of 110 tasks spanning gameplay mechanics, multiplayer behavior, AI and world orchestration, animation and movement, UI and session code, loading behavior, online-service integration, persistence, data serialization, XR behavior, and rendering-oriented plugins. These tasks require models to make native C++ changes that compile and satisfy behavioral tests within executable Unreal Engine projects. Across twelve evaluated configurations, the strongest model reaches 55.5\% pass@1, while 31 tasks remain unsolved by every configuration. Our results demonstrate that frontier coding agents continue to struggle with deeply integrated C++ development for real-time interactive software, highlighting game-engine benchmarks as a valuable complement to existing software engineering evaluations.

</details>


### [56] [Don't Blame the Large Language Model: How Scaffolding Evolution Shapes Coding Agent Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03691)
*Oussama Ben Sghaier,Hao Li,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次通过纵向对照实验，固定底层LLM不变，仅改变编排层（scaffolding）的版本，系统评估了35个连续版本的coding agent编排层对智能体效果和效率的影响，发现质量波动主要源于编排层的变更而非模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究几乎全部关注模型本身的质量变化，忽略了快速演进的编排层对agent质量的影响；实践中编排层变更引发的质量回退常被错误归因于模型。

Method: 首先对5个主流开源编排层（Codex、Qwen Code、Gemini、OpenCode、OpenHands）的版本发布历史进行实证分析，然后以Qwen Code CLI为对象，固定LLM不变，评估其35个连续版本在50个SWE-bench Verified任务上的表现，并追溯质量波动到具体的开发模式和架构组件。

Result: 发现编排层发布速度极快（超过每天2个版本），且质量波动与特定的开发模式（如PR变更）和架构组件直接相关，给出了具体的定性证据。

Conclusion: 编排层的快速演化是coding agent质量波动的重要来源，应在质量归因和系统设计中予以重视。

Abstract: Coding agents, autonomous systems that use large language models (LLMs) to resolve software engineering tasks, rely on agentic scaffolding: a middleware layer in between a developer and a large language model that orchestrates system prompts, tool execution, context management, and iterative reasoning loops. While these scaffoldings evolve at extreme velocities, no study has examined how this evolution affects agent quality (i.e., effectiveness and efficiency) over time. Practitioners regularly report quality regressions after scaffolding updates, yet consistently attribute them to the underlying model rather than the scaffolding itself. In this paper, we address this gap by conducting the first controlled longitudinal study that isolates the scaffolding's contribution. Unlike prior work that fixes the scaffolding and varies the model, we fix the model and vary only the scaffolding, evaluating 35 sequential releases to measure their impact on agent effectiveness and efficiency. We first empirically study the development and release evolution of five major open-source scaffoldings (i.e., Codex, Qwen Code, Gemini, OpenCode, and OpenHands), revealing extreme release velocities exceeding two releases per day and thousands of issues within months. We then perform a controlled deep dive into 35 sequential releases of the Qwen Code CLI, evaluating each against 50 stratified SWE-bench Verified tasks while holding the underlying LLM constant. We trace the resulting quality fluctuations to specific development patterns and architectural components, and illustrate our findings with concrete qualitative evidence linking individual pull requests to measured quality shifts.

</details>


### [57] [SkillFab: An Agent-Native Skill Production Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03780)
*Anjie Xu,Yifeng Cai,Yi Li,Zixing Wang,Zhiyu Zhang,Jingfan Chen,Ruohan Xu,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillFab是一个智能体原生平台，用于将缺失的能力转化为可审查、可复用的Agent技能，涵盖需求发现、代码仓库管理、Git提交审查、注册表发布以及通过Web/REST/MCP接口暴露的全生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent在执行任务时缺乏标准化的技能复用和审查机制，导致能力发现困难、开发流程不可追溯、长周期任务难以恢复和审查。

Method: 采用"先需求后实现"的模式，通过SkillFab管理的仓库、Git提交证据采集、维护者审查和注册表发布形成完整生命周期；通过Web、REST和MCP接口统一暴露。

Result: 实现了端到端OS检测技能运行、Docker研究包转化为可复用技能知识、以及外部优化案例的审查式提交流程三个案例。

Conclusion: SkillFab提供了一个让Agent技能开发可审查、可恢复、可复用的平台模型，弥合了Agent能力缺口与标准化技能管理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: SkillFab is an agent-native platform for turning missing capabilities into reviewed, reusable Agent Skills. At runtime, agents first search for reusable skills; when no adequate skill exists, the unmet capability becomes a demand-first issue before any repository or implementation branch needs to exist. Development then proceeds through a SkillFab-managed repository, Git-ingested commit evidence, maintainer review, and registry publication. The same lifecycle is exposed through web, REST, and MCP surfaces, so humans, scripts, and external agents operate on shared state rather than separate task logs. The current system uses scoped Git push URLs, native range commit ingestion, workflow-state reads, and workflow-event histories to make long-running agent work reviewable and recoverable. We document the platform model, architecture, implemented capabilities, and three case studies: an end-to-end OS-detect skill run, a Docker research package that converts operational practice into reusable skill knowledge, and an external optimization case showing how improved skill artifacts can enter SkillFab as reviewable, versioned submissions.
  Deployment: https://skillfab.ai.

</details>


### [58] [Refused in Chat, Written in Code: Workflow-Level Jailbreak Construction in IDE Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03968)
*Abhishek Kumar,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文揭示了LLM驱动的IDE集成编码代理在工作流层面存在安全漏洞：即使模型在单轮对话中完全拒绝有害请求，通过将有害目标拆解到软件开发的正常流程步骤（分解任务、生成文件、运行代码、多轮迭代）中，仍能成功实现有害目的。在GitHub Copilot上对四个闭源模型测试发现，基线条件下816次仅8次成功（完全拒绝），而工作流攻击下816/816全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM安全评估仍沿用聊天机器人范式（单轮有害提示→单轮响应），但实际部署的编码代理需要多轮交互完成任务。这种评估方式可能严重高估编码代理在实际工作流程中的安全性。

Method: 提出"工作流级越狱构造"方法，将有害目标拆解到软件开发工作流的正常阶段中。使用GitHub Copilot in VS Code，在Hammurabi's Code、HarmBench、AdvBench三个基准的204个提示上，对比直接聊天、CSV读取、单步代码修复三种基线条件与完整工作流条件下的模型行为。

Result: 基线条件下四个模型几乎完全拒绝（816次仅8次成功），而完整工作流条件下所有816次提示均成功生成不安全的教学补全，经两位专家按严格评分标准独立确认。

Conclusion: 对话式拒绝基准会严重高估已部署编码代理的安全性，需要设计能跨多轮IDE工作流及其生成产物进行安全推理的防御机制。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as IDE-integrated coding agents that decompose tasks, generate and edit files, run code, and refine outputs over many turns. Yet their safety is still often evaluated as if they were chatbots: one harmful prompt, one response, judged in isolation. We introduce workflow-level jailbreak construction, a failure mode in which a harmful objective is assembled across ordinary stages of a software-development workflow rather than generated through a single direct prompt. Using GitHub Copilot in Visual Studio Code, we study four closed-weight backends: Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Haiku 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Gemini 3.5 Flash. Across 204 prompts from Hammurabi's Code, HarmBench, and AdvBench , the models show near-complete refusal under direct chat, CSV-read, and single-step code-fix baselines, with only 8/816 successful responses in each baseline condition. Under the full workflow, however, the same prompts and backends produce 816/816 unsafe teaching-shot completions, all independently confirmed by two expert evaluators under a strict rubric. These results show that conversational refusal benchmarks can substantially overstate the safety of deployed coding agents and motivate defenses that reason about safety across multi-turn IDE workflows and their generated artifacts, not only individual chat turns.

</details>


### [59] [The "I Don't Know" Filter: Enhancing Agentic Reliability in Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04034)
*Stefan Broecker,Mason del Rosario,Boris Selitser,Thomas Strohmer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种考虑错误函数调用负面后果的智能体评估指标，以及一个轻量级可训练过滤器，用于量化语言模型的不确定性并移除潜在有害的函数调用，从而使智能体能够在不确定时表达"不知道"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在函数调用基准测试中表现快速提升，但训练和评估指标鼓励模型在不确定时也做出肯定回答，导致幻觉。在高风险应用中，这种幻觉可能造成灾难性后果。

Method: 1) 提出考虑错误函数调用负面后果的智能体评估指标；2) 设计轻量级可训练过滤器，量化语言模型的不确定性，检测并抑制不确定的函数调用，而无需修改底层模型。

Result: 展示了让智能体在不确定时学会说"不知道"的实用路径，提高了生产环境的可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种提高语言模型智能体在函数调用场景下可靠性的方法，通过评估指标和不确定性过滤器来减少幻觉，这对于生产环境中的可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: The language models that underpin agents have seen a rapid rise in performance on function calling benchmarks. However, the metrics used in the training and evaluation of these models often encourage models to make positive claims even when the answer is uncertain, leading to hallucinations. Such hallucinations can be disastrous when language models are trusted to use function calls to make decisions in high stakes applications. To that end, we propose an agent evaluation metric that takes into account the negative outcomes associated with incorrect function calls. Further, to catch hallucinations before they can cause harm, we propose a lightweight trainable filter that can quantify a language model's uncertainty and remove potentially harmful function calls. By training that filter to detect and suppress uncertain function calls without modifying the underlying model, we demonstrate a practical path toward agents that know when to say "I don't know," a property we argue is essential to production reliability.

</details>


### [60] [Kaizen: Metamorphic Fuzzing and Differential Testing for LLM-Translated HPC Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04058)
*Oscar Ludwig,Ninad Anklesaria,Zheming Jin,Swaroop Pophale,Kausar Moshood,Christian J. DeVore,Brandon Gill,Cassius Villareal,Keita Teranishi,Manish Motwani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了Kaizen框架，利用蜕变模糊测试和差异测试来评估LLM翻译HPC代码（如CUDA转OpenMP）的正确性，发现编译成功不能保证语义正确，且全程序翻译比内核级翻译困难得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估LLM翻译HPC代码的方法（编译成功、token级相似度、静态基准测试）无法可靠保证行为正确性，需要更有效的正确性评估框架。

Method: Kaizen框架：通过源代码变异进行蜕变模糊测试生成语义等价程序，基于语法的输入模糊测试探索行为多样性，利用差异测试暴露原始程序与翻译程序之间的语义差异。

Result: 在16个科学应用、7个领域上的评估表明：编译成功不能作为正确性的代理指标；LLM翻译程序存在系统性编译错误模式；语义错误往往与输入相关，需差异测试才能暴露；全程序翻译比内核级翻译困难得多。

Conclusion: 需要面向正确性的LLM辅助HPC代码翻译评估方法，Kaizen框架能有效发现编译成功但语义错误的翻译。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to port scientific codes across heterogeneous high-performance computing (HPC) programming models, such as translating CUDA to OpenMP, OpenACC, Kokkos or SYCL. However, current evaluations use compilation success, token-level similarity, or developer-written tests from static benchmarks, which cannot reliably ensure behavioral correctness. We present Kaizen, a metamorphic fuzzing and differential testing framework for evaluating the correctness of LLM-translated HPC code. Kaizen uses metamorphic fuzzing via source-code mutation to generate semantically equivalent programs, grammar-based input fuzzing to explore behavioral diversity, and differential testing to expose semantic divergences between original and translated applications that compile and pass developer-written tests yet produce incorrect scientific results. We evaluate Kaizen on CUDA-to-OpenMP translation of 16 scientific applications from seven domains using three fine-tuned LLMs at kernel-level and full-program granularity. Our evaluation reveals that (1) compilation success is a poor proxy for correctness; (2) LLM-translated programs exhibit systematic compile-time error patterns, with nine categories for kernel-level translation and 27 for full-program translation; (3) semantic errors that survive compilation are often input-dependent and require differential testing to expose; and (4) full-program translation is substantially harder than kernel-level translation. These findings highlight the need for correctness-oriented evaluation of LLM-assisted HPC code translations.

</details>


### [61] [Correct but Slow: An Empirical Study of the GPU Kernel Evaluation Gap in Modern Domain-Specific Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04454)
*Tingxi Li,Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究了Triton和TileLang等GPU DSL内核的"正确性-性能差距"问题，发现基于正确性的评估会遗漏性能极差的内核（如TileLang LayerNorm比PyTorch慢300倍），并提出轻量级筛选检查（相对效率与roofline利用率）来检测此类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DSL内核评估仅依赖基于参考实现的数值验证来确认正确性，但一个功能正确的内核可能在吞吐量上远低于它要替代的优化库算子，这种正确性-性能差距未被系统研究。

Method: 在NVIDIA A100和GH200 GPU上使用22个来自五种算子类别的Triton和TileLang内核，通过正确性检查、性能基准测试、瓶颈分析以及两种轻量级检查（库相对效率与roofline利用率）进行系统性实验。

Result: 1) 正确性评估会遗漏严重性能问题（如TileLang LayerNorm慢300倍）；2) 不同内核家族的瓶颈原因不同（归一化/归约类多是可修复的编写缺陷，卷积/GEMM类存在代码生成和自动调优覆盖限制）；3) 库相对效率与roofline利用率联合可检测所有低效内核，并区分可修复缺陷与结构性残留问题。

Conclusion: 仅依赖正确性评估不足以判断DSL内核是否适合替代库算子，需要补充性能导向的轻量级检查来识别低效内核，并为不同性能瓶颈提供针对性优化指导。

Abstract: Modern GPU domain-specific languages (DSLs), such as Triton and TileLang, are increasingly used to implement specialized deep-learning kernels and as target languages for automated kernel-generation systems. Existing DSL-kernel evaluations establish correctness through reference-based numerical validation -- necessary, but silent on replacement quality: a functionally valid kernel may still fall far below the throughput of the optimized library operator it is intended to replace.
  We study this correctness-performance gap using 22 Triton and TileLang kernels from five operator categories on NVIDIA A100 and GH200 GPUs, asking whether correctness-based evaluation identifies kernels unsuitable as library replacements, why such failures occur, and how they can be detected without exhaustive benchmark coverage. The study yields three results. \emph{First}, correctness-based evaluation can admit severe slowdowns: an idiomatic TileLang LayerNorm kernel passes KernelBench's correctness check while running more than 300$\times$ slower than the PyTorch baseline. \emph{Second}, the causes differ by kernel family. TileLang normalization and reduction slowdowns are mainly repairable authoring defects, such as sequential reductions and unnecessary dtype conversions, whereas convolution and large general matrix multiplication (GEMM) retain residual gaps after optimization due to code-generation and autotuning-coverage limits; vendor-library algorithm selection contributes only marginally. \emph{Third}, two lightweight checks -- library-relative efficiency and roofline utilization -- are complementary screening criteria: together they flag every functionally valid but inefficient kernel in our suite and separate repairable authoring defects from structural residuals.

</details>


### [62] [Obey, Diverge, Collapse: Blind Obedience to Incorrect Instructions Drives Code LLMs to Irrecoverable Code Semantic Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04537)
*Raj Jaiswal,Anany Singh Divy,Savar Bhasin,Adi Bajpai,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究代码语言模型在面对错误指令时的行为，发现模型能够识别指令错误但依然会盲从执行，导致引入难以恢复的语义错误，这种现象被称为"盲从"。该问题在单次和迭代修复场景中均存在，且扩展推理无法逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试假设代码语言模型接收到的指令都是正确的，但实际生产中指令可能包含错误。作者希望探究模型是否会抵抗或服从错误指令，以及这带来的后果。

Method: 使用RunBugRun数据集（算法Python问题+确定性测试用例），设计四个实验，在单次和迭代修复场景中评估代码语言模型对错误指令的抵抗或服从行为。

Result: 模型能正确识别指令错误，但仍然会服从执行；这种服从导致引入超出原始bug的额外错误；后续自引导迭代修复无法恢复被破坏的代码状态；扩展推理无法逆转该现象。

Conclusion: 发现了传统通过率评估无法捕捉的行为特性——盲从与幽灵错误，这对部署在生产环境中的代码语言模型有直接影响。

Abstract: Code language models are now trusted collaborators in production workflows for debugging, refactoring, and iterative repair, and every benchmark that evaluates them assumes the instructions they act on are correct. We study what happens when that assumption breaks. We evaluate code language models across four experiments designed to assess whether models resist or obey incorrect instructions in single-pass and iterative repair settings, using the RunBugRun dataset of algorithmic Python problems with deterministic test cases. Our findings reveal a striking behavioral pattern: models correctly identify an incorrect instruction as wrong, then follow it anyway. This compliance unknowingly introduces errors beyond the original bug, and the corrupted code state cannot be recovered through subsequent self-guided iterative repair, which fails to converge across passes. We term this Blind Obedience, characterize the Ghost (Unknown) Errors it introduces, quantify the proportion of cases where semantic corruption proves irrecoverable, and show that extended reasoning cannot reverse it. These findings surface behavioral properties invisible to pass-rate evaluation, with direct consequences for code language models deployed in production settings.

</details>


### [63] [LLM-Driven CI-CD Workflow Intelligence for Cyber Systems Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04579)
*Bonan Shen,Jiazhou Gao,Tao Ning,Wei-Jung Huang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LLM的CI/CD分析管道，从59,550个高星GitHub仓库中收集127,559个配置文件，检测到434,769个反模式问题，并通过少样本提示为每个仓库平均生成8.25条YAML有效的推荐，论证了CI/CD可观测性需要结合诊断、上下文和人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD工作流已成为可执行的操作策略，但现有的工作流挖掘仅关注阶段标签识别，无法判断工作流是否脆弱、是否与生态系统异常或是否需要优先修订。

Method: 构建LLM-based CI/CD分析管道，包含仓库富集、反模式检测、阶段挖掘和推荐生成四个模块，对59,550个GitHub高星仓库中的34,225个CI/CD项目进行大规模分析。

Result: 在75,201个工作流中检测到434,769个反模式发现，以可靠性和可维护性问题为主；阶段使用在不同语言间存在显著差异；移动项目在发布和缓存使用上更突出；少样本提示平均生成8.25条推荐，96.1%为YAML有效。

Conclusion: CI/CD可观测性应结合诊断、上下文和人工审查，而非仅将工作流挖掘视为阶段分类问题。

Abstract: CI/CD workflows have become executable operational policy: they decide what gets built, tested, released, and deployed, and they mediate how maintainers interact with delivery infrastructure. That makes them an important measurement point for cyber-systems engineering. Recent large language model (LLM) work shows that workflow stages can be recognized directly from configuration files, but stage labels alone do not tell us whether a workflow is brittle, unusual for its ecosystem, or worth revising first. We present an LLM-based CI/CD analysis pipeline that combines repository enrichment, anti-pattern detection, stage mining, and recommendation generation over a large GitHub corpus. Starting from 59,550 repositories with at least 1,000 stars, we identify 34,225 projects with CI/CD and collect 127,559 configuration files. Across 75,201 analyzed workflows, the anti-pattern detector reports 434,769 findings, dominated by reliability and maintainability issues. Across 59,906 configurations, stage usage differs significantly by language ($χ^2 = 4168.88$, $p < 0.001$, Cramer's $V = 0.063$), and domain analysis shows distinct operational profiles, including higher release and cache usage in mobile projects. For repository-level recommendation generation, few-shot prompting performs best overall, averaging 8.25 recommendations per repository with 96.1% YAML-valid snippets. Taken together, the results argue for CI/CD observability that combines diagnosis, context, and human review rather than treating workflow mining as a stage-classification problem alone.

</details>


### [64] [Dashboard2Code: Evaluating Multimodal Models on Reconstructing Interactive Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04727)
*Tianhao Niu,Ziyu Han,Qiguang Chen,Shiqi Zhou,Baocai Shan,Hengjie Fang,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Dashboard2Code任务，要求模型通过交互式探索自动生成仪表盘代码，并构建了DashboardMimic基准（180个Plotly+Dash仪表盘-代码对）及自动化评估框架。实验表明现有模型在高复杂度仪表盘上表现不佳，开源与闭源模型间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动数据可视化生成方法主要关注静态图表，忽略了实际数据探索中广泛使用的交互式仪表盘，缺乏让模型通过主动交互来生成仪表盘代码的研究。

Method: 定义了Dashboard2Code任务（模型需主动探索交互式仪表盘、从交互中获取反馈并生成复现代码）；构建了DashboardMimic基准（180个Plotly+Dash仪表盘-代码对，三种难度等级，八种交互模式）；提出了结合代码语义分析与动态交互测试的自动化评估框架。

Result: 最强系统在高复杂度仪表盘上仍表现困难；开源与闭源模型在Dashboard2Code任务上存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: 交互式仪表盘生成是极具挑战的任务，现有多模态大模型在此任务上能力有限，尤其开源模型差距明显，亟需进一步研究。

Abstract: Automatic data visualization generation has advanced rapidly with multi-modal large language models, yet existing efforts largely focus on static charts and overlook the interactive dashboards commonly used for real-world data exploration. We introduce Dashboard2Code, a novel task that requires a model to proactively explore an interactive dashboard, acquire and integrate feedback from its own interactions (e.g., clicking and filtering), and generate code that reproduces the target dashboard. To support comprehensive evaluation, we present DashboardMimic, the first Plotly+Dash benchmark for Dashboard2Code, comprising 180 carefully designed and manually verified dashboard-code pairs spanning three difficulty levels and covering eight common real-world interaction patterns. We further propose an automated evaluation framework tailored to dashboards that combines code semantic analysis with dynamic interaction-based testing to assess visual and interaction consistency, showing strong agreement with human judgments. Experiments across a range of open- and closed-source multi-modal models reveal that even the strongest systems struggle on high-complexity dashboards and that a substantial performance gap remains between open-source and closed-source models on the Dashboard2Code task.

</details>


### [65] [Cam2Sim: Neural Scenario Reconstruction for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04770)
*Davide Jannussi,Stefano Carlo Lambertenghi,Constantin Carste,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Cam2Sim是一个将真实世界驾驶录像转化为可回放的CARLA仿真场景的工具，通过高斯泼溅渲染缩小仿真与真实视觉差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真测试的有效性受限于合成仿真输出与真实相机观测之间的差距，需要弥合这一视觉差异以提高自动驾驶系统测试的真实性。

Method: 从相机图像和位姿出发，重建道路几何、自车轨迹、静态车辆和仿真资产，并利用高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）渲染接近原始录像的相机观测。支持ROS数据提取、停车车辆检测、OpenStreetMap地图生成、CARLA场景构建、轨迹回放及闭环执行。

Result: 在真实城市驾驶场景中，基于高斯泼溅的渲染相比标准仿真渲染减少了视觉差距，并且提升了与真实世界参考运行的行为相似度。

Conclusion: Cam2Sim工具可有效缩小仿真与真实之间的视觉差距，提升自动驾驶仿真测试的逼真度和有效性。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing enables safe and repeatable evaluation of autonomous driving systems, but its effectiveness is limited by the gap between synthetic simulator outputs and real-world camera observations. To address this problem, we present Cam2Sim, a tool that transforms real-world driving recordings into playable CARLA simulation scenarios. Starting from camera images and poses, Cam2Sim reconstructs road geometry, ego trajectories, parked vehicles, and simulation assets, and augments the reconstructed environment with Gaussian Splatting to render camera observations that resemble the original recording. The framework supports ROS-based data extraction, parked-vehicle detection, OpenStreetMap-based map generation, CARLA scenario construction, Gaussian Splatting training, trajectory replay, and closed-loop execution with a system under test. We validate Cam2Sim on a real-world urban-driving scenario with a camera-based end-to-end driving model, comparing reconstruction quality, image-generation quality, and closed-loop behavior against both a simulation-only baseline and the real-world target. Results show that Gaussian-Splatting-based rendering reduces the visual gap with respect to standard simulator rendering and improves behavioral similarity to the real-world reference runs. The artifact is publicly available at https: //github.com/ast-fortiss-tum/cam2sim, and a screencast showing the tool is available at https://youtu.be/KmZ74l1__lI

</details>


### [66] [Teaching LLMs a Low-Resource Language: Enhancing Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04939)
*Kilian Kier,Alessandro Giagnorio,Omar AbedelKader,Oleksandr Zaitsev,Robert Peharz,Romain Robbes,Gabriele Bavota,Stéphane Ducasse*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了将LLM代码补全引入低资源语言Pharo的实践经验，构建了从数据整理、继续预训练到微调的端到端管道，并设计了专门的基准测试。实验表明，针对Pharo特化的小型模型在代码补全精度上显著超越原始基座模型和更大的通用LLM，且足以支持IDE实时补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在主流语言中代码补全表现出色，但低资源语言（如Pharo）因训练数据稀缺，代码补全工具质量滞后（Pharo IDE仅支持单token补全），需要探索如何将LLM能力迁移到这类语言。

Method: 构建端到端管道：(1) Pharo特定数据整理（从GitHub仓库、包管理器等收集并标准化代码数据）；(2) 对开源代码LLM进行继续预训练（持续预训练）；(3) 在Pharo代码补全任务上微调。同时设计了两个基准测试：语法学习基准和真实仓库masked代码补全基准。

Result: Pharo特化模型在代码补全精度上显著优于原始基座模型，且超过规模更大的通用代码LLM的Pharo补全准确率。模型足够小，可在IDE中实现实时补全。

Conclusion: 该案例证明了即使对于低资源编程语言，通过针对性的数据整理和模型特化（继续预训练+微调），也可以将强LLM代码补全能力带到该语言社区，且小模型即可达到实用水平。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) unlocked new possibilities in automated code writing, becoming the backbone of most code completion tools. While LLMs excel in mainstream languages, they often lack support for the so-called low-resource languages where training data is scarce. As a result, these languages lag behind in the quality of code completion tooling available to their communities. A concrete example is Pharo, a Smalltalk-inspired language whose IDE currently offers only single-token completion. In this work, we report on our experience bringing LLM-based code completion to Pharo. First, we describe an end-to-end pipeline that combines Pharo-specific data curation, continued pre-training and fine-tuning of open code LLMs. Second, we introduce a set of Pharo code completion benchmarks designed to evaluate whether models (i) learn Pharo's syntax and (ii) accurately complete masked Pharo code from real-world GitHub repositories. Third, we show empirically that Pharo-specialized models substantially outperform their original base checkpoints and also exceed the accuracy of substantially larger code LLMs on Pharo completion. Overall, our case study demonstrates the feasibility of bringing strong LLM-based code completion to low-resource programming languages, with models small enough to provide ``real-time'' in-IDE support.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [67] [Training Hybrid Block Diffusion Language Models with Partial Bidirectionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02805)
*Pranshu Chaturvedi,Parth Shroff,Tarun Suresh,Hangoo Kang,Kaiyue Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种BDLM Mamba-Attention混合架构，通过限制反向Mamba扫描到活跃去噪块来实现精确缓存，从而在长上下文生成中大幅提升吞吐量（65K tokens时比全序列DiffuMamba-H快19.7倍，262K tokens时比BDLM attention快3.7倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文生成受限于内存带宽而非计算能力，现有混合扩散模型（如DiffuMamba）在块级扩散时无法精确缓存状态，导致效率低下。本文旨在解决反向Mamba扫描不能前缀缓存的问题。

Method: 提出BDLM Mamba-H混合架构，核心创新是将反向Mamba扫描限制在活跃的去噪块内，而非扫描整个序列，从而使得每块的KV状态可以精确缓存并在块间复用。

Result: 87M参数下C4-en验证困惑度最优；350M参数与BDLM attention竞争力相当。长上下文推理吞吐量：65K tokens时达全序列DiffuMamba-H的19.7倍，262K tokens时达BDLM attention的3.7倍。

Conclusion: Mamba混合架构是长上下文扩散模型的有潜力的方向，通过限制反向扫描范围实现缓存复用，显著提升吞吐量。

Abstract: High-throughput long-context generation is one of the central challenges for large language models. Generation is typically memory-bandwidth-bound rather than compute-bound: each decoding step must stream the accumulated key/value (KV) cache from memory, so bandwidth demand grows with context length while only one token is emitted. Two parallel approaches have therefore emerged: reducing memory access with efficient attention variants and linear-time mixers such as Mamba, or increasing parallel computation by generating blocks of tokens at once. However, technical challenges arise when combining these two ideas. Earlier hybrid diffusion models such as DiffuMamba use bidirectional Mamba mixing, including a reverse-direction scan relative to causal generation. This reverse scan needs to scan the entire sequence, so its states are not prefix-only and cannot be precisely reused as a cache even when diffusion is performed block by block. We propose a BDLM Mamba--attention hybrid that addresses this challenge by restricting the reverse Mamba scan to the active denoising block, which enables exact caching across blocks. In an 87M-parameter DCLM sweep, BDLM Mamba-H achieves the best C4-en validation perplexity compared to BDLM attention and full-sequence baselines. At 350M parameters, it remains competitive with BDLM attention. For long-context inference, BDLM Mamba-H reaches 19.7x the throughput of full-sequence DiffuMamba-H at 65K tokens and 3.7x the throughput of BDLM attention at 262K, showing that Mamba hybrids are a potential long-context diffusion architecture.

</details>


### [68] [Variable Bit-width Quantization: Learning Per-Group Precision for "Bigger-but-Smaller" Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02893)
*Hamish Ogilvy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VBQ（可变比特宽度量化）是一种训练时方法，让语言模型中每连续64个权重通过Gumbel-Softmax学习自己的比特数（1/2/4/8位），发现高度不均匀的分配模式（如69%的组坍缩到1位），并将其冻结为固定配方。该配方在相同存储下可容纳更大模型（如131M模型1.82平均位在3.8x更小存储下击败55M FP16），且通过自定义内核加速推理（最高4.7x），其机制是深层逐步自愈浅层的量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特量化将精度视为全局超参数，每层或每个权重使用相同比特宽度，无法捕捉模型内部不同部分对精度的差异化需求，导致比特资源分配效率低下。

Method: 将每连续64个权重组视为一个可学习精度的单元，通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛从{1,2,4,8}中选择比特数，采用交替优化使精度logits获得任务对齐的梯度信号，训练后冻结发现的分配模式作为固定配方。

Result: VBQ发现高度异质的精度分配（69%的组为1位，LM头仅1.09位，首个MLP块约2.5位）；131M模型1.82平均位在TinyStories上PPL 4.2，优于55M FP16（PPL 4.4）且存储减少3.8x；1.46B模型匹配593M FP16质量，存储减少3.7x；推理加速1.9x（1.0B）到4.7x（9B）；深层自愈量化误差。

Conclusion: 精度应被视为可学习的非均匀资源，非均匀分配比特预算比均匀分配更高效；VBQ开辟了"更大但更小"的模型规模范式，但超过1.5B参数的经济性搜索仍有待解决。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization shrinks language models but treats precision as a single global hyper-parameter: every weight uses the same bit-width. We introduce Variable Bit-width Quantization (VBQ), a training-time method in which each contiguous group of 64 weights learns its own resolution from {1,2,4,8} bits via a Gumbel-Softmax relaxation, trained jointly by an alternating optimization that gives the precision logits a clean, task-aligned signal. VBQ discovers a consistent, strongly heterogeneous allocation within individual projection types, not merely across layers, impossible to express with per-layer methods: 69% of groups collapse to 1 bit, the LM head averages 1.09 bits, while the first MLP block keeps ~2.5 bits. This pattern is stable enough to freeze into a fixed recipe and reuse without further search. The recipe yields a "bigger-but-smaller" regime: a 131M model at 1.82 mean bits reaches perplexity 4.2 on TinyStories, beating a 55M FP16 model (PPL 4.4) at 3.8x less storage, and lets a 1.46B model on FineWeb-Edu match a 593M FP16 control at ~3.7x less storage with 2.5x more parameters. As quality-per-byte, VBQ is 3.9-8.4x more efficient than FP16. The recipe maps directly to packed low-bit storage, so it also accelerates inference: with custom fused dequantize-and-multiply kernels, memory-bandwidth-bound autoregressive decode is faster at equal output, and the speedup grows with scale (parity at 131M, 1.9x at 1.0B, 4.7x at 9B on Apple silicon). A distributional analysis (KL divergence and argmax-flip rate) reveals a striking mechanism: deeper layers progressively self-heal the quantization error injected by early layers. The win is a from-scratch, train-time phenomenon; scaling the search economically beyond 1.5B parameters remains open. VBQ reframes precision as a learnable, non-uniform resource and shows that spending a fixed bit budget unevenly beats spending it uniformly.

</details>


### [69] [Rank-Order N-of-M Codes for Sparse Distributed Memory: Disentangling Representation and Learning Effects in Noise Robustness Against Contemporary Neuromorphic Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02967)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估秩次N-of-M编码作为CALM中阈值二值编码的替代方案，验证了WheelSDM与RankOrderSDM的等效性，发现RankOrderSDM在容量上优于StandardSDM最多13.4个百分点，并揭示了鲁棒性提升主要来自编码与MAX-Hebbian学习的交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CALM（Nechesov and Ruponen, 2025）指出其阈值二值编码器是一个开放的设计问题，亟需探究更优的编码方案以提升稀疏分布式记忆（SDM）在大型语言模型持续学习中的表现。

Method: 通过忠实重实现验证CALM架构，设计多种子容量实验和BER鲁棒性实验，在GloVe-100和句子嵌入上测试，并进行组件级能耗分析。

Result: 1) 确认WheelSDM与RankOrderSDM完全等价（余弦相似度1.0000）；2) RankOrderSDM在缩配置下饱和点超越StandardSDM 13.4个百分点；3) 鲁棒性增益主要来自秩次编码与MAX-Hebbian学习的交互，编码器单独贡献有限；4) 理想秩次编码在4位精度下能耗仅为SpikingMamba SI-LIF神经元的一半。

Conclusion: 秩次N-of-M编码为SDM提供了明确收益，但编码与学习机制的协同设计才是关键；对CALM等记忆增强AI架构有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Large language models remain limited as continual learning systems, motivating renewed interest in Sparse Distributed Memory (SDM) as an explicit online episodic memory. CALM (Nechesov and Ruponen, 2025) identifies its threshold-binary encoder as an open design question. This paper evaluates rank-order N-of-M encoding (Furber et al., 2007) as an alternative. We make three contributions. First, a faithful reimplementation validates the published architecture by confirming exact equivalence between WheelSDM and RankOrderSDM (cosine similarity 1.0000 across 10 seeds) and reproducing the documented divergence of RDLIF neurons under interference. Second, multi-seed capacity experiments show RankOrderSDM outperforming StandardSDM by 13.4 percentage points at saturation in the scaled configuration and by 0.8 percentage points at the published architecture scale. Third, BER robustness experiments disentangle representation and learning effects, showing that the large robustness gain arises primarily from the interaction of rank-order encoding with MAX-Hebbian learning, while the encoder alone provides only a small advantage under matched learning conditions. Experiments on GloVe-100 embeddings confirm this small but consistent encoding benefit on real structured data, whereas sentence embeddings exhibit a ceiling effect at low memory load. A secondary analysis shows that idealized rank-order encoding requires half the component-level encoding energy of SpikingMamba's SI-LIF neurons at four-bit precision, although decoder costs dominate overall system energy. These results identify which components of the original rank-order SDM architecture provide measurable benefits for contemporary memory-augmented AI systems, offering practical guidance for architectures such as CALM.

</details>


### [70] [Can Model Merging Improve Aggregation in DiLoCo?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03011)
*Stefan Horoi,Benjamin Thérien,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立局部SGD/DiLoCo的伪梯度聚合与任务算术模型合并之间的类比关系，将多种模型合并方法应用于分布式优化，发现Iso-C方法优于简单平均和动量DiLoCo，并提出带Nesterov动量的IsoLoCo方法，在大语言模型预训练中显著优于DiLoCo，且优势随工作节点数增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信高效的分布式训练方法（如局部SGD、DiLoCo）在节点数增多或本地步数增加时性能下降，而模型合并技术在处理异构模型聚合方面表现优异，本文试图将后者引入前者以提升通信受限场景下的分布式训练效果。

Method: 首先建立局部SGD伪梯度聚合与任务算术模型合并的类比关系，在分布式训练框架中替代简单平均聚合；系统评估多种SOTA模型合并方法（如Task Arithmetic、TIES、Iso-C等）；基于效果最好的Iso-C提出IsoLoCo，为其配备Nesterov动量，在语言模型预训练任务上验证效果。

Result: Iso-C聚合在DiLoCo框架下优于简单伪梯度平均和动量DiLoCo；IsoLoCo在不同节点数、模型规模和内部步数下均显著优于DiLoCo，且优势随节点数增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 受模型合并启发的聚合策略是低通信分布式训练的有效方案，IsoLoCo可作为DiLoCo的替代方法，尤其适用于大规模分布式场景。

Abstract: Model merging techniques, which aggregate independently finetuned models into one to combine their capabilities, have become a topic of significant interest in recent years, with a broad array of methods having been proposed to tackle this problem. Simultaneously, an emerging trend in distributed learning has been the use of methods such as local SGD and DiLoCo, which greatly reduce communication costs by periodically aggregating the independently trained local models. However, these communication-efficient methods have been shown to degrade in performance relative to the FLOP-matched data-parallel gold standard as the number of independent local models grows and as the number of local training steps before global communication is increased. In this work, we draw an explicit analogy between the pseudo-gradient aggregation step in local SGD/DiLoCo and task arithmetic-based model merging, establishing a straightforward way to utilize merging methods in the context of distributed optimization. We then evaluate multiple state-of-the-art model merging methods in this setting and identify one method in particular, Iso-C, as a promising approach for improving DiLoCo. We find that DiLoCo SGD with Iso-C aggregation outperforms not only simple pseudo-gradient averaging but even the momentum-based DiLoCo, despite lacking a momentum mechanism itself. Building on this finding, we propose IsoLoCo, which adapts Iso-C for distributed training by equipping it with Nesterov momentum. Our empirical evaluations on language model pre-training across varying numbers of local workers show that IsoLoCo significantly outperforms DiLoCo, with the gap between them widening as the number of workers increases. This advantage remains present across model sizes and inner step counts, confirming that merging-inspired aggregation is an effective strategy for low-communication distributed training.

</details>


### [71] [OmniFocus: Query-Guided Modality-Balanced Token Compression for Omni-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03050)
*Shijie Cao,Qingyu Zhang,Boxi Yu,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniFocus，一种无需训练的查询引导token压缩方法，用于全模态大语言模型，通过对视频和音频进行独立重要性估计实现模态对称压缩，在低token保留率下保持性能并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大语言模型处理音视频输入时产生大量token序列，导致推理成本高昂；现有压缩方法依赖单模态引导，忽略了查询相关证据的时序局部性，且假设两种模态信息密度分布对齐，存在模态偏差问题。

Method: 提出OmniFocus，一种无需训练的查询引导token压缩方法。对视频和音频进行独立重要性估计，采用模态对称压缩设计，保留各模态特有的关键证据同时维持音视频对齐，避免单模态引导压缩带来的模态偏差。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni模型系列上的四个音视频基准测试中，OmniFocus在25% token保留率下优于现有基线；在DailyOmni上保持59.40准确率，同时实现最高1.38倍prefill加速。

Conclusion: OmniFocus实现了良好的精度-效率权衡，是一种实用的推理加速方案。

Abstract: Omni modal large language models (OmniLLMs) have attracted wide attention for their ability to jointly process audio and video, but they generate large token sequences under audio-visual inputs, leading to substantial inference cost. Existing audio-visual token compression methods often rely on unimodal guidance, overlooking the temporal locality of query-relevant evidence in audio-visual inputs and implicitly assuming that the two modalities share a temporally aligned information density distribution.
