We propose \textbf{OmniFocus}, a training-free query-guided token compression method for OmniLLMs that performs independent importance estimation for video and audio, enabling a modality-symmetric compression design that preserves modality-specific salient evidence while maintaining audio-visual alignment, thereby mitigating the modality bias issue that can arise from unimodal-guided compression. Experiments on the Qwen2.5-Omni model family across four audio-visual benchmarks show that OmniFocus maintains strong compressed performance at low token retention ratios and outperforms existing baselines on several major benchmark scores at 25\% token retention. On DailyOmni with Qwen2.5-Omni-7B at 25\% token retention, OmniFocus maintains 59.40 accuracy while delivering up to 1.38$\times$ prefill speedup relative to the full-token baseline, highlighting a favorable practical accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [72] [STELLA: Efficient Sensor-to-LLM Translation for On-Device Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03089)
*Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Albert Zomaya,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STELLA是一个高效的传感器到LLM的翻译框架，通过轻量级分层分词器将多通道惯性数据压缩为紧凑的潜在传感器令牌，在冻结的预训练LLM上进行活动识别，支持设备端个性化，在7个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的HAR方法难以在边缘设备上部署：原始传感器提示过长、云端推理带来延迟和隐私风险、微调LLM将其变为特定任务分类器。需要一种能在边缘设备上高效运行且保护隐私的解决方案。

Method: 提出STELLA框架，核心是轻量级分层分词器，将多通道惯性窗口压缩为固定数量的紧凑潜在传感器令牌，投影到冻结预训练LLM的嵌入空间，与自然语言提示结合进行标签评分。支持仅更新轻量级分词器的设备端个性化，并利用本地检索上下文增强推理。

Result: 在7个公共HAR数据集和8个基准设置上达到SOTA，比先前方法提升高达11.83% F1；设备端个性化可额外提升21.91% F1；在移动和边缘设备上实现实时推理。

Conclusion: 高效的传感器分词是实现边缘设备上准确、隐私、个性化LLM-based HAR的实用路径。

Abstract: HAR is increasingly expected to run continuously on edge devices, yet recent LLM-based methods remain hard to deploy: raw sensor prompts are long, cloud inference adds latency and privacy risk, and fine-tuned LLM pipelines turn general-purpose models into task-specific classifiers. We present STELLA, an efficient sensor-to-LLM translation framework for on-device HAR that shifts the burden from LLM adaptation to sensor tokenization. A lightweight hierarchical tokenizer compresses an entire multi-channel inertial window into a fixed set of compact latent sensor tokens, which are projected into the embedding space of a frozen pretrained LLM and combined with a natural-language prompt for label scoring. This preserves activity-relevant temporal and cross-channel structure while keeping LLM-side computation predictable across sensor configurations. STELLA also supports on-device personalization, adapting only the lightweight tokenizer on small amounts of user-specific labelled data and augmenting inference with a local retrieval context, keeping the LLM, user data, and retrieval on device. Across seven public HAR datasets and eight benchmark settings, STELLA achieves new state-of-the-art performance, improving over prior methods by up to 11.83% F1; on-device personalization yields up to a further 21.91% F1 as user data accumulates after deployment. STELLA also outperforms representative time-series tokenizers under the same LLM pipeline and achieves real-time inference under practical mobile and edge budgets, showing that efficient sensor tokenization is a practical path toward accurate, private, and personalized LLM-based HAR on edge devices.

</details>


### [73] [Heterogeneous Graph Condensation via Role-Aware Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03097)
*Fuyan Ou,Yulin Hu,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HGC-RC，一种角色感知的异构图压缩框架，通过轻量传播嵌入提取、角色感知混合聚类（类分区聚类+无监督类型聚类）和高效图重构，在保持任务性能的同时加速大规模异构图上的HGNN训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构图神经网络（HGNN）在大规模异构图上的训练计算开销巨大，现有图压缩方法主要针对同构图且依赖昂贵的梯度匹配或双层优化，难以实用化扩展到异构场景。

Method: 1) 轻量传播提取语义增强的节点嵌入；2) 角色感知混合聚类：对标记目标节点采用类分区聚类以保持类别分布，对非目标节点采用无监督类型聚类以保留跨类型连接；3) 基于聚类分配高效重构压缩异构图。

Result: 在多个数据集上，HGC-RC优于现有最先进基线，显著加速HGNN训练且不损失任务性能。

Conclusion: HGC-RC提供了一条实用的途径来加速大规模异构图上HGNN的训练，为图压缩在异构场景下的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks (HGNNs) have exhibited remarkable efficacy in modeling complex systems with multiple types of nodes and relations, yet their training on large-scale heterogeneous graphs remains computationally prohibitive. Although graph condensation methods can effectively improve learning efficiency on large-scale graphs, existing condensation processes are mainly designed for homogeneous graphs and typically rely on computationally expensive gradient matching or bilevel optimization paradigms, rendering them impractical for heterogeneous settings. To address these limitations, we propose HGC-RC, a simple yet effective role-aware heterogeneous graph condensation framework. Specifically, HGC-RC first extracts semantically enhanced node embeddings via lightweight propagation. It then introduces a role-aware hybrid clustering strategy consisting of class-partitioned clustering for labeled target nodes to preserve class distributions and unsupervised type-wise clustering for non-target nodes to retain critical cross-type connectivity. Finally, a compact heterogeneous graph is efficiently reconstructed based on the resulting cluster assignments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HGC-RC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, offering a practical pathway to accelerate HGNN training on large-scale heterogeneous graphs without sacrificing task performance

</details>


### [74] [Adaptive Inference Batching using Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05272)
*Ruslan Sharifullin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习（RL）在推理服务系统中学习自适应批处理和路由策略的能力。实验发现，在单GPU场景下，调优后的静态批处理策略已接近最优，RL提升有限（+0.1%~1.0%）；而在多GPU异构路由场景中，RL学到的负载隔离策略消除了队头阻塞，相比Round-Robin提升348%，比最强启发式基线（最短队列）提升48%，吞吐量提高60%、延迟降低25%，且能泛化到未见过的突发和真实流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理服务系统需要在突发、异构工作负载下平衡吞吐量和延迟，但业界仍采用需要手动调优且无法适应流量变化的静态批处理策略。作者希望探索RL能否学习自适应批处理和路由策略以超越这些启发式方法。

Method: 将问题建模为关于队列状态、请求类型和GPU可用性的MDP；在离散事件模拟器上训练REINFORCE和PPO智能体，模拟器基于排队理论和生产轨迹（Azure Functions, BurstGPT）验证；评估覆盖标准泊松流量、极端突发、真实轨迹和异构多GPU路由场景。

Result: 单GPU场景：调优的静态批处理策略在泊松流量下已接近最优，RL仅带来边际提升（+0.1%~1.0%）。多GPU异构路由场景：RL发现负载隔离策略，消除队头阻塞，相比Round-Robin提升348%，比最短队列启发式提升48%，吞吐量提高60%、延迟降低25%，且满足SLA约束。注意力增强策略网络相比MLP收敛速度快约20%。

Conclusion: RL的优势集中在组合性、多资源决策问题中，而非单资源的时间调度问题。这一边界条件有助于判断在生产推理基础设施中引入学习型策略是否值得其工程成本。

Abstract: Inference serving systems must balance throughput and latency under bursty, heterogeneous workloads, yet the industry standard remains static batching policies that require manual tuning and cannot adapt to shifting traffic. We investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) can learn adaptive batching and routing policies that outperform these heuristics, training REINFORCE and PPO agents on a discrete-event simulator validated against queuing theory and production traces (Azure Functions, BurstGPT). We formulate the problem as an MDP over queue state, request type and GPU availability, evaluating across standard Poisson traffic, extreme bursts, real-world traces and heterogeneous multi-GPU routing.
  Our central finding is a clear boundary condition for RL's value in systems problems. In single-GPU settings, a well-tuned static batching policy is already near-optimal under Poisson-like arrivals and RL offers only marginal gains (+0.1% to +1.0%). In multi-GPU heterogeneous routing, however, where fast and slow requests compete for shared resources, the agent discovers a workload-segregation policy that eliminates Head-of-Line blocking, yielding a 3.5x (348%) improvement over Round-Robin and a 48% improvement over the strongest heuristic baseline (Shortest-Queue), with 60% higher throughput and 25% lower latency while respecting SLA constraints. The policy generalizes to unseen bursty and real-world traffic despite training only on synthetic Poisson arrivals and an attention-augmented policy network converges roughly 20% faster than an MLP baseline.
  These results suggest RL's advantage over engineered heuristics concentrates in combinatorial, multi-resource decisions rather than single-resource temporal scheduling, a practical distinction for deciding where learned policies justify their engineering cost in production inference infrastructure.

</details>


### [75] [Rethinking Neural Nonlinearity as Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03148)
*Muhammad Sabih,Frank Hannig,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Threshold Gating (TG)原始操作，证明常见激活函数（ReLU、SiLU、Softmax等）均可统一为输入条件分支门的特例，并提出最小分支定理，TG可统一映射到模拟存内计算硬件实现，解决ADC/DAC瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活函数种类繁多，硬件实现不统一，特别是模拟存内计算系统中ADC/DAC转换器是功耗和面积的瓶颈，需要一个统一的硬件友好的激活原语。

Method: 提出Threshold Gating (TG)原始操作，将各种激活函数（分段线性、平滑、Softmax等）统一为输入条件分支门的形式，并证明最小分支数定理。

Result: 在CNN、Transformer、RNN等架构上验证了等价转换，保持性能无需重新训练；从零训练可获得模型压缩、性能提升和训练加速效果。

Conclusion: TG是一个统一、硬件友好的激活原语，在模拟存内计算中可减少ADC/DAC开销，降低功耗和面积。

Abstract: Activation functions are considered an essential primitive for neural nonlinearity, i.e., they enable neural networks to serve as universal approximators. In this paper, we show that this nonlinearity can also be achieved by input-conditioned threshold gating through branches as a universal primitive. We demonstrate that standard activations -- whether piecewise-linear (ReLU, PReLU, Hardtanh) or smooth (SiLU, Sigmoid, Tanh, GELU) -- are in fact instances of a single Threshold Gating (TG) primitive. For softmax, we show that it admits an exact TG conversion via its equivalent per-element Sigmoid form. We then validate these equivalences by converting pretrained networks across CNNs, transformer-based models, and recurrent architectures, preserving model performance without requiring retraining. Threshold Gating also enables training from scratch that goes beyond replacing existing activations, enabling gains in model compression, performance, and shorter training. We also propose a 'Minimal Branch Theorem' which relates the minimum number of required branches in our primitive to the trainability of general deep neural networks. In terms of hardware implementation, TG maps to a unified implementation in the case of analog in-memory systems, addressing the bottleneck of analog-to-digital and digital-to-analog converters (ADC/DAC) that is known to significantly impact power consumption and on-chip area.

</details>


### [76] [From Global to Local: Efficient Regional Weather Downscaling with Global Weather Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03279)
*Wiktor Kamzela,Jakub Kubiak,Adam Dobosz,Jędrzej Miczke,Anatol Kaczmarek,Piotr Wyrwiński,Wojciech Stefaniak,Wojciech Kotłowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预训练天气基础模型的降尺度框架，通过在潜在空间中添加轻量级多尺度预测头，实现对全球预报的区域细化，在不重新训练的情况下将分辨率提升两个数量级，计算成本远低于传统数值天气预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限区域模型计算成本高，而基于学习的超分辨率方法忽略跨尺度的统计和物理不匹配问题，需要更高效的区域天气预测方法。

Method: 在预训练天气基础模型骨干网络上添加轻量级多尺度预测头，直接在潜在空间中进行区域细化，以区域数值模拟为训练目标，并利用地面气象站观测评估系统偏差。

Result: 在大多数指标上优于数值天气预报（NWP），计算成本仅为NWP的一小部分；基于全局预训练天气基础模型潜在空间的降尺度能力优于标准图像超分辨率方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的基础模型驱动降尺度框架能够高效实现高分辨率区域天气预测，且预训练模型潜在空间比传统超分辨率方法更适合降尺度任务。

Abstract: Accurate regional weather prediction requires resolving fine-scale structure while remaining consistent with global dynamics. Traditional limited area models rely on computationally expensive simulations, while many learning-based approaches frame the problem as super-resolution, overlooking statistical and physical mismatches across scales. We propose a foundation-model-driven downscaling framework that learns regional refinements of global forecasts by augmenting a pretrained weather model backbone with lightweight, multi-scale prediction heads operating directly in its latent space. Despite being trained on substantially coarser inputs, the pretrained backbone supports regional adaptation at resolutions corresponding to a two-order-of-magnitude increase in grid-cell resolution, without the need for retraining. The proposed approach uses regional numerical simulations as training targets and is evaluated not only against gridded datasets but also against ground-based weather station observations, enabling analysis of systematic biases between global reanalysis, regional simulations, and in-situ weather station observations. Our experiments show improved accuracy in comparison to NWP on most of the metrics at the fraction of computational cost. Moreover, we observe that building on a latent space of globally pre-trained weather foundation model offers better downscaling capabilities than the standard image-based super-resolution approaches.

</details>


### [77] [No Time Like the Present: Agentic Test-Time Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03441)
*Yanbo Wang,Jinhua Hao,Yuze Shi,Kun Yuan,Ming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT)，一种在LLM Agent多轮交互过程中进行连续测试时训练的方法，通过对重复n-gram token降权来缓解自我训练中的漂移问题，并在vLLM上实现低开销的并发服务系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在长轨迹中性能会下降（重复探索、重复失败动作、丢失有效策略），现有TTT方法多为单次适应，无法应对多轮交互中策略变化导致的自我训练漂移问题。

Method: 提出aTTT，在token级别对出现在先前更新中重复n-gram的token进行降权，保留新token全权重；同时基于vLLM的运行时LoRA API构建并发服务系统，实现低开销的在线更新。

Result: 在ALFWorld上提升最多5.0个点，在SWE-bench Lite上提升最多4.9个点；开销仅为无TTT的1.9倍；效果集中在模型已有能力但因长轨迹而漂移的场景。

Conclusion: aTTT主要通过保持模型已有能力而非教授新能力来提升性能，为长周期Agent任务提供了一种实用的在线自适应方案。

Abstract: LLM agents often degrade over long episodes: as trajectories grow, they revisit explored states, repeat failed actions, and lose strategies that previously worked. Test-time training (TTT) offers a way to adapt model weights to the evolving task state, but existing LLM TTT methods largely adapt once to a fixed input. We study continuous TTT in multi-turn agent episodes, where each update changes the policy that generates later training text. This creates a self-training loop that helps when new trajectory information appears, but can amplify drift when the agent gets stuck and repeatedly trains on similar text. We find that update-text repetition distinguishes these regimes and introduce Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT), a token-level reweighting method that downweights the loss on tokens appearing in repeated $n$-grams from prior updates while leaving novel tokens fully weighted. To run such updates inside live episodes, we build a concurrent serving system using vLLM's runtime LoRA API, limiting overhead to 1.9$\times$ the no-TTT cost. aTTT improves success by up to 5.0 points on ALFWorld and 4.9 points on SWE-bench Lite. The gains concentrate where models already have task competence but drift over long trajectories, suggesting that aTTT mainly preserves existing competence rather than teaching new abilities.

</details>


### [78] [Co-Adaptive Multi-Task LoRA: Transfer-Aware, Label-Free Control of Domain Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03522)
*Wei Zhang,Lin Tang,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDA，一种协同自适应控制器，通过无标签前向传播获取每个领域的胜任度信号和跨领域亲和度，动态调整多任务LoRA微调中各领域的参与权重（损失权重+采样比例），在异构领域上相比均匀混合、学习混合、梯度手术等方法提升平均性能且仅用一半数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效微调流水线大多在固定均匀混合上训练，未能回答两个关键问题：每个领域应参与多少，以及哪些领域应共同训练（存在正迁移和干扰）。

Method: 通过共享适配器对少量无标签探针数据的前向传播，获取每个领域的胜任度信号（跟踪剩余空间和学习速度）和跨领域亲和度（预测成对迁移效果）；将这些信号纳入CoDA控制器，求解一个熵正则化的二次规划问题，动态调整每个领域的损失权重和采样比例，奖励高剩余空间、仍在学习、相互协同的领域，抑制干扰领域。

Result: 在五个异构领域和两个骨干网络上，CoDA相比均匀混合、学习混合、梯度手术多任务优化器和在线数据选择方法，平均性能提升且仅用一半数据，同时降低了跨领域梯度冲突。

Conclusion: 证明了胜任度信号跟踪领域风险、参与规划有唯一收缩不动点、其解执行迁移感知的水填充；分析、消融和对照实验验证了各环节的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a single low-rank adapter on many domains at once is multi-task learning: the domains must be co-learned, and how they share the adapter decides whether they help or hurt one another. Most efficient fine-tuning pipelines ignore this and train on a fixed, uniform mixture, leaving two coupled questions unanswered: how much should each domain participate, and which domains should be co-trained given that some transfer positively and others interfere? We show that both answers can be read off cheaply and without labels. A forward pass of the current shared adapter over a small unlabeled probe yields, per domain, a competence signal whose level tracks remaining headroom and whose trajectory tracks learning speed; the drift of these probe representations yields a signed cross-domain affinity that predicts pairwise transfer. We fold both into CoDA, a co-adaptive controller that solves a small entropy-regularized quadratic program on the simplex to set each domain's participation -- jointly its loss weight and its share of the sampled data -- rewarding high-headroom, still-learning, mutually synergistic domains and damping interfering ones. The controller is forward-only, adds no trainable parameters, and wraps any multi-task LoRA pipeline. Across five heterogeneous domains and two backbones, CoDA improves the average over uniform mixing, learned mixtures, gradient-surgery multi-task optimizers, and online data selection while using half the data, and lowers cross-domain gradient conflict. We prove that the competence signal tracks domain risk, that the participation program has a unique fixed point reached by a contraction, and that its solution performs transfer-aware water-filling; analysis, ablations, and controls corroborate each claim.

</details>


### [79] [Differentiate the Evaluator, Not the Program: An Efficient Runtime Representation for Neuro-Symbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03574)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出原生可微分虚拟机(NDVM)，通过将符号结构与可微分数值状态分离，在运行时直接对可执行程序进行自动微分，避免了为每个候选程序编译独立计算图的开销，实现了程序与参数的联合搜索加速约24倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI科学建模中，程序与参数的联合搜索面临瓶颈：候选程序需要内部梯度优化才能评估，现有方法要么为每个程序构建独立可微图（丢失程序即数据特性），要么使用解释器方式（解释器开销主导计算）。需要一种既能保持程序作为运行时数据又能高效进行梯度计算的方法。

Method: NDVM将符号结构（标签、符号、环境、控制流）保留为原生运行时数据，而将数值载荷组织为密集批处理缓冲区，沿实际执行轨迹记录精确反向模式梯度。这样一次求值器遍历即可分摊到大量参数向量上，实现批量化自动微分。

Result: NDVM与参考后端在前向和梯度上等价，每通道批处理分摊约60倍加速，近线性多核扩展，支持两个独立前端。在LLM提出的程序上进行固定预算联合搜索时，NDVM在墙钟时间上约24倍更快达到高质量解。

Conclusion: 运行时微分可作为科学发现工作流的实用系统基础，显著加速程序与参数联合搜索。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly propose executable scientific models whose value depends on both their symbolic structure and their fitted continuous parameters. This makes parameter calibration the bottleneck of program-and-parameter co-search: an outer loop can generate thousands of candidate programs, but each needs an inner gradient-based optimization before it can be assessed. Staging each candidate into its own differentiable graph makes individual models fast but sacrifices the program-as-data property that keeps search fluid; interpreter-based approaches preserve programs as runtime data but pay interpreter overhead that dominates the numerical work.
  We present the Native Differentiable Virtual Machine (NDVM), a runtime representation that differentiates executable programs without compiling each candidate into a separate graph. NDVM separates symbolic structure from differentiable numeric state: tags, symbols, environments, and control remain native runtime data, while numeric payloads live in dense batched buffers with exact reverse-mode gradients recorded along the realized execution trace, so one evaluator walk is amortized across large populations of parameter vectors. A locked cost model of a real differentiable self-hosted Scheme interpreter motivates the design. We realize NDVM as a native runtime with forward and gradient equivalence to the reference backend, about 60x per-lane batch amortization, near-linear multicore scaling, and two independent front ends. In fixed-budget co-search over LLM-proposed programs, NDVM reaches high-quality solutions about 24x sooner in wall-clock time, suggesting runtime differentiation as a practical systems foundation for scientific discovery workflows.

</details>


### [80] [FedACT: Federated Adaptive Coordinate Trust Modulation for Robust Transformer Training under Data Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03763)
*Shuai Li,Qinglin Wang,Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Hongyang Hu,Haotian Mo,Yigui Feng,Ziang Liu,Qisong Xiao,Jie Liu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedACT，一种用于联邦 AdamW 训练的全局感知坐标信任调制方法，通过在坐标级别重新分配更新幅度来解决联邦 Transformer 训练中的坐标信任不匹配问题，在视觉 Transformer、CNN、LLM 预训练和微调任务上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦 Transformer 训练中广泛使用局部 AdamW，但其自适应更新在异构客户端数据下存在坐标级别的信任不匹配问题，现有联邦自适应优化器主要在客户端更新或通信轮次层面处理不匹配，仍然在所有坐标上密集均匀地应用校正后的自适应方向。

Method: FedACT 首先形成全局校正的自适应方向，然后根据坐标级别的信任分数重新分配更新幅度：为同时得到局部梯度和全局校正支持的坐标分配更大步长，同时在其余坐标上保留较小的非零更新。

Result: 在联邦视觉 Transformer、CNN、LLM 预训练和 LLM 微调上的大量实验表明，FedACT 持续优于强联邦自适应基线，在更强的数据异质性下对 Transformer 模型提升最大。机制分析表明 FedACT 改善了跨客户端的梯度方向一致性。

Conclusion: 坐标级别的信任分配有效补充了轮次级别的全局-局部校正，FedACT 是一种有效的联邦 AdamW 训练优化方法。

Abstract: Federated Transformer training increasingly relies on local AdamW, whose adaptive updates can provide much stronger local progress than SGD-based training. However, under heterogeneous client data, even globally corrected AdamW updates may remain highly uneven in coordinate-wise reliability. We refer to this phenomenon as coordinate trust mismatch. Existing federated adaptive optimizers mainly address mismatch at the client-update or communication-round level, but still apply the corrected adaptive direction densely and uniformly across coordinates. In this paper, we propose FedACT, a global-aware coordinate trust modulation method for federated AdamW training. FedACT first forms a globally corrected adaptive direction and then reallocates update magnitudes according to a coordinate-wise trust score, assigning larger steps to coordinates jointly supported by local gradients and global correction, while preserving smaller non-zero updates on the remaining coordinates. Extensive experiments on federated vision Transformers, CNNs, LLM pre-training, and LLM fine-tuning show that FedACT consistently improves over strong federated adaptive baselines, with the largest gains on Transformer models under stronger data heterogeneity. Mechanism analyses further show that FedACT improves cross-client direction consistency, suggesting that coordinate-level trust allocation effectively complements round-level global-local correction. Code will be released.

</details>


### [81] [AdaptiveSD A Stability-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive Speculative Decoding Framework with Multi-Policy Orchestration for CPU-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03876)
*Sadra Saremi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaptiveSD 是一个完全运行时自适应的推测解码框架，旨在确保在严重内存带宽约束下（如纯CPU实现）可靠地部署小型量化GGUF语言模型，通过运行时监控引擎、自适应草稿控制器、动态策略引擎和KV缓存协调层四个组件协同工作，避免传统固定深度推测解码导致的性能下降或系统崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型量化GGUF模型用于端侧推理时，固定深度推测解码在实践中常因带宽饱和、不稳定甚至灾难性资源耗尽导致系统故障，亟需一种能可靠交付这些模型的方案。

Method: 提出AdaptiveSD框架，包含四个组件：运行时监控引擎跟踪多个信号，自适应草稿控制器执行十一规则策略层次（优先保障系统资源），动态策略引擎使用启发式和强化学习动态调整策略，KV缓存协调层通过INT8影子缓冲区和位置感知驱逐管理缓存。

Result: 论文未在摘要中展示具体实验结果，但强调从单纯追求吞吐量转向评估浪费的草稿计算、令牌间延迟离散度等指标来衡量有效性。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSD能够确保跨模型类型和工作负载的稳健、可靠执行，优于传统仅关注吞吐量的方法。

Abstract: With the rise of small quantized GGUF-based language models and their increasing use for on-device inference tasks, we have seen the growing need for an approach capable of reliably delivering these models at scale even under severe memory bandwidth constraints such as those imposed by pure CPU implementations. Fixed-depth speculative decoding has emerged as one promising technique, but in practice, it often leads to performance degradation due to either bandwidth saturation, instability, or even catastrophic resource exhaustion resulting in system failure. To overcome this problem, we introduce AdaptiveSD, a fully runtime-adaptive speculative decoding framework aimed at ensuring robust, reliable execution across the spectrum of model types and workloads. Our solution consists of four tightly-coupled components working together in a continuous feedback loop: a Runtime Monitoring Engine tracking multiple signals relevant to ongoing computation, an Adaptive Draft Controller enforcing an eleven rule policy hierarchy prioritizing system resource preservation over raw draft count, a Dynamic Policy Engine employing a suite of heuristic and reinforcement learning techniques to dynamically modify policies depending upon workload behavior, and finally, a KV Cache Coordination Layer managing cache states with fine-grained control through INT8 shadow buffers and position aware evictions. While conventional approaches focus solely on maximizing throughput, we instead assess the effectiveness of our approach based on several key metrics including wasted drafted compute and inter-token latency dispersion alongside standard measures of speculative efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [Auto: The AGI Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04542)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍Auto编译器，它能将LLM agent的运行时行为记录、分析、提取确定性部分并编译为WebAssembly认知二进制，通过分层运行时和守卫机制实现低成本、高可靠性的Agent执行，在AUTO-BENCH上达到87.1%的确定性检测率，边际成本从59降至2微美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM agent每次运行都需要从头开始通过前沿模型逐token生成行为，成本高、速度慢、效率低下，需要一种方法将重复性、确定性的行为固化下来以避免重复计算。

Method: 提出Auto编译器，包含：(1) 记录agent实时行为；(2) 测量并提取确定性部分为验证程序或蒸馏专家；(3) 编译为WebAssembly认知二进制；(4) 分层运行时通过校准守卫执行，守卫触发则回退到参考agent并重新编译。

Result: 在AUTO-BENCH上，560条记录中87.1%的frontier-agent片段被判定为确定性的；在300项流式任务中，经过3次分布偏移，端到端边际成本降低6.4倍（从59到2微美元），准确率达96.9%。

Conclusion: 校准和参考agent的保真度（而非模型能力）决定了低成本方案能否保持正确性；松散的守卫会导致48.9%的错误，而不忠实的参考agent会导致验证门拒绝重新编译。

Abstract: Every LLM agent run re-derives its behavior token by token on a frontier model: brilliant, expensive, slow, and unbounded. We present Auto, a compiler that records live agent behavior, measures which parts are secretly deterministic, extracts them into verified programs or distilled specialists, and emits cognition binaries: WebAssembly artifacts whose manifests carry measured guarantees and whose declared capabilities are physically enforced by the sandbox. A tiered runtime executes compiled behavior behind conformally calibrated guards; guard trips deopt to the reference agent, and the captured trace recompiles back down, so nothing is figured out twice. We use "AGI compiler" in one narrow, testable sense: a system that autonomously converts novel experience into permanent, verified, near-free skill while measuring what it does not know. On AUTO-BENCH, a benchmark we introduce and pre-register, 87.1% of 560 recorded frontier-agent spans are witnessed-deterministic (three of the four censused task families measure 100.0%). On a 300-item stream with three scheduled distribution shifts, the closed loop compiles three artifact generations and drives marginal cost from 59 to 2 micro-dollars per item (6.4x end-to-end) at 96.9% parity on witnessed inputs with zero errors. The same stream also quantifies the failure modes: a loose guard silently mislabels 48.9% of compiled answers, and an unfaithful deopt reference causes the verification gate to refuse recompilation. Calibration and reference fidelity, not model capability, decide whether cheap stays correct. Code: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/auto

</details>


### [83] [Beyond the Need for Speed: Energy-Aware Code Generation via Simulation-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04577)
*Saurabhsingh Rajput,Tushar Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Green Tea语料库（基于确定性架构仿真替代硬件能耗测量），通过能量对比对的监督微调+闭环强化学习（GRPO）训练能耗感知的代码模型，并提出CARET指标（同时惩罚牺牲正确性的优化）。在143个问题上实现12.63%的CARET提升，击败58.4%的人类专家参考代码，同时揭示IPC指标在67.8%的问题上会错误评估能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型只关注功能正确性，忽略了软件能效；硬件功耗测量存在方差大、不可复现的问题，无法为训练提供规模化、可重复的反馈信号。

Method: 1) 用确定性架构模拟器替代硬件测量，构建Green Tea语料库；2) 在能量对比对上进行监督微调；3) 使用模拟器在环的GRPO强化学习；4) 提出CARET指标联合评估正确性与能效。

Result: 模拟在环pipeline在143个held-out问题上实现12.63% CARET提升（近三倍于纯微调），58.4%有效输出超越人类专家能效。IPC指标在67.8%问题上会错误排序真实能效。

Conclusion: 直接能量模拟对于优化代码能效不可或缺；论文开源了数据集和基础设施，节省26.3万CPU小时的复现成本，推动社区部署节能代码生成模型。

Abstract: Code models strictly prioritize functional correctness, leaving software energy efficiency as an unoptimized byproduct. Training models to generate energy-efficient code requires reproducible feedback at scale, which physical hardware measurement cannot reliably provide due to variance.
  In this paper, we replace hardware profiling with a deterministic architectural simulation harness to build Green Tea, a corpus of $3.5$ million evaluations across $1{,}474$ C++ problems. We train an energy-aware code model via supervised fine-tuning on energy-contrastive pairs, followed by closed-loop reinforcement learning (GRPO) using simulation-in-the-loop feedback. To rigorously evaluate deployment readiness, we introduce the Correctness-Adjusted Reduction in Energy Total (CARET), a metric that explicitly penalizes code that sacrifices functionality for efficiency.
  On $143$ held-out problems, our simulation-in-the-loop pipeline achieves $12.63\%$ CARET, nearly tripling the gain of fine-tuning alone, and successfully beats the energy efficiency of human-expert references on $58.4\%$ of its valid outputs. Furthermore, our analysis exposes the IPC trap: standard throughput proxies like Instructions-Per-Cycle (IPC) actively misrank true energy efficiency on $67.8\%$ of problems, proving the absolute necessity of direct energy simulation. By releasing our dataset and infrastructure, we bypass the $263{,}000$ CPU-hours required for reproduction, structurally empowering the community to deploy inherently energy-efficient code generation models.

</details>


### [84] [Knowing When to Stop: Predicting Execution-Consistency Convergence in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03991)
*Yaron Anavi,Mor Aisenberg,Nadav Nesher,Elena Khabibullina,Isabella Cattinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级一维模型，通过观察重复LLM调用的运行一致性轨迹，动态决定何时停止Text-to-SQL的置信度估计，从而在保证收敛预测可靠性的前提下减少不必要的LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Text-to-SQL置信度估计需要多次调用LLM并观察结果一致性，但何时停止（即一致性已收敛）是一个未解决的问题。现有方法缺乏自适应停止机制，导致要么过早停止（置信度不准），要么过晚停止（浪费计算资源）。

Method: 将停止问题建模为收敛预测问题，训练一组轻量级1-D模型。模型观察运行一致性轨迹，在每一步判断继续运行是否可能显著改变当前一致性。同时利用运行间弱序列相关性，通过排列顺序作为训练增强（带可调shuffling权重）。基准对比包括Beta-Bernoulli停止规则和基于运行次数的基线。

Result: 在BIRD基准和两个生产客户数据集上，该方法能自适应调整停止点：一致性提前收敛时提前停止，收敛较晚时继续运行。性能在三个数据集上保持一致。在注入噪声模拟不完美评判器的实验中，该方法仍能可靠预测收敛。

Conclusion: 重复LLM调用的运行一致性轨迹可以有效地预测收敛，轻量级模型能够实现自适应停止决策，在保证可靠性前提下显著降低计算开销。该方法对噪声具有鲁棒性，适用于生产环境。

Abstract: Repeated LLM calls are the standard way to estimate how trustworthy a Text-to-SQL result is: run the pipeline multiple times, judge each SQL execution, and use the consistency of the verdicts as a confidence signal. The open question is when to stop, when the consistency has converged. We formulate this as a convergence-prediction problem and train a family of lightweight 1-D models that observe the running consistency trajectory and decide, at each step, whether further runs are unlikely to shift it materially, and we benchmark them against a principled Beta-Bernoulli stopping rule and a learned run-count baseline. On the BIRD benchmark and two production customer datasets, our method adapts its stopping point to each user question, halting sooner when consistency converges early and continuing longer when it converges late. We further show that the weak serial correlation between runs lets us permute their order as a training augmentation, controlled by a tunable shuffling weight. Performance stays consistent across the three datasets, and to mimic an imperfect production judge we inject noise into the correct/incorrect verdicts obtained by comparing the generated and ground-truth SQL results, showing that the method still predicts convergence reliably.

</details>


### [85] [Directional Curvature from Armijo Backtracking: A Low-Cost Sharpness Probe and a Calibration-Free Learning-Rate Safeguard for Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03998)
*Ashmitha R,Jörg Frochte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Armijo回溯线搜索中的接受步长α来低成本估计损失曲面的局部锐度（最大Hessian特征值λ₁），以此作为Adam优化器的学习率安全上限，仅需一次初始化探测（约1%开销）即可防止发散，且无需在训练中重复探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量损失曲面的局部锐度（最大Hessian特征值λ₁）对确定最大稳定梯度步长至关重要，但传统方法（Lanczos或Hessian-vector乘积迭代）计算成本高。作者希望找到一种低成本、在线可用的方法获取这一信息，以提升优化器的鲁棒性。

Method: 观察到Armijo回溯线搜索中的接受步长α与方向曲率q = gᵀHg/‖g‖²之间存在乘法带关系，进而logα与logλ₁高度相关（Pearson -0.91至-0.95）。利用初始化时的一次Armijo探测得到的α推导出学习率上限，将其作为Adam的"safeguard"（保护机制），而非加速优化器。还提出沿Adam更新方向进行探测以消除架构依赖的标定需求，使用固定安全系数κ=2即可。

Result: 该方法使Adam在10⁻³到3.0三个数量级的学习率范围内均保持鲁棒，开销仅约1%。一次初始化探测足够，训练中重复探测无额外收益。沿Adam更新方向探测时，固定安全系数κ=2可在9种架构和4个基准的全部学习率网格上避免发散，且可直接迁移到AdamW。

Conclusion: Armijo回溯线搜索中的步长信息可低成本估计损失曲面的最大曲率，据此设置学习率上限能显著提升Adam的鲁棒性，且方法简洁高效（约1%开销），是一种实用的AI基础设施优化技术。

Abstract: The local sharpness of the loss, the top Hessian eigenvalue $λ_1$, determines the largest stable gradient step, but measuring it normally requires Lanczos or Hessian-vector iterations. We observe that a single Armijo backtracking line search already carries this information at the cost of a few forward passes: the accepted step $α$ brackets the \emph{directional} curvature $q = g^\top H g/\|g\|^2$ within the multiplicative band set by the backtracking factor. Across CIFAR-10, Fashion-MNIST and Imagenette, $\logα$ tracks $\logλ_1$ at Pearson $-0.91$ to $-0.95$, giving a low-cost online Edge-of-Stability reading. Used once at initialisation, this measurement yields a learning-rate cap (a safeguard, not a faster optimiser) that makes Adam robust to a too-large initial learning rate across more than three orders of magnitude ($10^{-3}$ to $3.0$), at about one percent overhead, and it is a no-op when the chosen rate is already safe. One probe is enough: periodic in-training probing adds no robust benefit. The raw-gradient probe exposes the mechanism but needs a safety factor calibrated to the architecture by a one-minute divergence sweep. Probing along Adam's own update direction removes this calibration: a single fixed safety factor $κ= 2$ avoids divergence on all nine architectures we test and across the full learning-rate grids of all four benchmarks, and the recipe transfers to AdamW unchanged.

</details>


### [86] [OmniOpt: Taxonomy, Geometry, and Benchmarking of Modern Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04033)
*Siyuan Li,Jiabao Pan,Yumou Liu,Zhuoli Ouyang,Xin Jin,Xinglong Xu,Jingxuan Wei,Shengye Pang,Jintao Che,Xuanhe Zhou,Conghui He,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniOpt 是一个统一的优化器调查和基准测试手册，通过五阶段元流水线、标准化线性最小化预言机、双维度分类法和跨领域基准测试，为大规模模型训练中的优化器选择提供系统化坐标体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模型训练的优化器选择已成为受计算、内存、调优预算和任务多样性共同约束的系统级设计决策，然而超过一百种优化器的方法论仍然碎片化，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 提出四大组件：1) 五阶段元流水线将优化器更新视为结构化变换；2) 使用范数约束线性最小化预言机统一不同优化器；3) 构建双维度分类法（机制家族 + 可测量训练目标）；4) 跨领域基准测试涵盖代表性优化器、模型规模和训练范式。

Result: 建立了统一的分类法和基准测试框架，系统分析了各方法家族在多项目标上的效果权衡。

Conclusion: 为研究社区提供了在显式机制和目标假设下选择优化器的操作坐标体系，并为优化器社区的未来发展指明方向。

Abstract: Optimizer selection for large-scale model training has become a system-level design decision constrained jointly by compute, memory, tuning budget, and task diversity, yet the landscape of over one hundred methods remains fragmented. We therefore present OmniOpt, a unified survey and benchmark cookbook of optimizers for the research community. OmniOpt rests on four coupled components. First, we treat every optimizer update as a structured transformation through a five-stage meta-pipeline, and show that most methods engage only one or two of these stages. Second, we use norm-constrained linear minimization oracles (LMOs) to unify different optimizers. Third, these two views ground a dual-dimension taxonomy, one dimension assigning each method to a mechanism family and the other recording the measurable training objectives it aims to improve. Fourth, and at the core of this paper, we instantiate the full taxonomy in a unified cross-domain benchmark spanning representative optimizers, model scales, and training regimes from language model pretraining to image classification, systematically analyzing each method family across multiple effect objectives and laying out their trade-offs. OmniOpt thus supplies the research community with an operational coordinate system for selecting optimizers under explicit mechanism and objective assumptions, and charts a direction for the future development of the optimizer community.

</details>


### [87] [Parametric Memory Decoding for Zero-Shot Routing in LoRA-Based External Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04118)
*Fengxian Ji,Zhuohan Xie,Jingpu Yang,Fan Zhang,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parametric Memory Decoding (PMD)框架，将零样本LoRA路由重新定义为对外部参数化记忆的解码过程，并基于此实现PMDRouter，通过单次基模型预填充的响应幅度对LoRA进行评分，在构建的PMD-Bench基准上取得了最优的内部信号零样本路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA路由方法往往引入额外的训练、部署和维护开销，而零样本LoRA路由方法在EPM设置下存在评估分散在不同任务设置中、路由信号缺乏统一视角的问题，因此需要一种无需维护额外路由组件的零样本LoRA路由方法。

Method: 提出PMD框架，将零样本LoRA路由重新定义为解码激活过程；基于PMD实例化PMDRouter，通过单次基模型预填充后各LoRA的响应幅度进行评分路由。

Result: 在构建的PMD-Bench（涵盖文档级、领域级知识和任务技能三个维度）上，PMDRouter在多个零样本路由设置中取得了最强的内部信号性能。

Conclusion: 零样本LoRA路由是可行的，PMD可作为改进零样本路由方法的通用框架。

Abstract: With the rise of parametric memory, LoRA-based External Parametric Memory (EPM) has emerged as a modular solution, but existing routing methods often introduce additional training, deployment, and maintenance overhead. This raises a natural question: can a LoRA-based EPM bank be routed without maintaining an additional routing component? However, existing zero-shot LoRA routing methods still face two problems under the EPM setting: (1) their evaluations are scattered across different task settings rather than organized around EPM access, and (2) their routing signals lack a unified perspective to guide systematic improvement. To address these problems, we organize PMD-Bench, covering document-level, domain-level knowledge, and task-skill, and propose Parametric Memory Decoding (PMD), the first framework designed to systematically improve zero-shot LoRA routing by reframing it as decoding activations over external parametric memory. Based on PMD, we further instantiate PMDRouter, which scores each LoRA by its response magnitude from a single base-model prefill. Experiments on PMD-Bench show that PMDRouter achieves the strongest internal-signal performance across multiple zero-shot routing settings. These results demonstrate the feasibility of zero-shot LoRA routing and suggest that PMD can serve as a general framework for improving zero-shot routing methods. Sources: Github (https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Parametric-Memory-Decoding-872A/)

</details>


### [88] [HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302)
*Hui Dong,Yanzhao Li,Jie Gao,Chunlu Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Sun,Zhenyuan Chen,Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFA4 是一种面向昇腾 NPU 的 4 位 HIF4 Cube GEMM 后训练算子级设计，通过 Smooth-QK 和 P-Reordering 两种机制在 FlashAttention 中执行低精度 QK^T 和 PV 运算，同时保持 FP16 online softmax 状态，在多个 LLM 上降低了精度损失和决策漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在昇腾 NPU 上高效部署 LLM 推理时，FlashAttention 中的 QK^T 和 PV 计算存在精度与性能的权衡问题。直接使用 HIF4 量化会导致较大的精度损失和决策漂移，需要一种算子级优化方案来缩小与 BF16 的差距。

Method: 1) Smooth-QK：对 RoPE 后的 Q 和 K 进行校准静态的每通道等效重缩放，将量化难度从 K 转移到 Q，避免推理时的逐 tile 在线约减。2) P-Reordering：从量化后的注意力权重 P_hat（而非高精度重建）累积 softmax 归一化因子，并将其融合到 PV Cube GEMM 中，尽管存在不一致性导致的输出缩放误差，但可通过分析进行补偿。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上恢复 37.5% 的精度差距，样本加权精度损失从 1.12 pp 降至 0.70 pp，MMLU 预测不一致率从 16.3% 降到 8.2%，MMLU 精度回归减少 57%。Gemma2-9B 上保持 0.7 pp 内差距。仅使用 P-Reordering 的模型上 MMLU 回归减少 41-52%。初步分析预计相对 BF16 关键路径延迟减少 35.4%。

Conclusion: HiFA4 是首个面向昇腾 HIF4 的 FlashAttention 算子级低精度设计，在保持推理效率提升潜力的同时有效缩小了量化精度差距，但片上硬件验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: We present HiFA4, a post-training operator-level design that executes both QK^T and PV in FlashAttention as 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMMs for LLM inference on Ascend NPUs, while maintaining the online softmax state in FP16. To our knowledge, HiFA4 is the first Ascend-HIF4-targeted design of this kind evaluated on standard NLP benchmarks.
  HiFA4 combines two mechanisms. Smooth-QK applies a calibration-static per-channel equivalent rescaling to Q and K after RoPE, transferring quantization difficulty from K to Q without per-tile online reduction at inference. P-Reordering accumulates the softmax normalizer from the same quantized attention weights P_hat used in the PV GEMM, rather than from a higher-precision reconstruction. We show that this inconsistent formulation introduces a coherent output-scaling error, and validate the effect on a Qwen3-8B Layer-0 MMLU trace, where all 3.6M measured attention tiles exhibit net probability-mass loss with median epsilon_bar = -0.064. P-Reordering also allows the normalizer to be fused into the PV Cube GEMM.
  Across five LLMs, HiFA4 reduces quantization-induced decision drift. On Qwen3-8B, it recovers 37.5% of the accuracy gap introduced by direct HIF4 quantization, narrows the sample-weighted accuracy loss from 1.12 pp to 0.70 pp, reduces BF16-inconsistent MMLU predictions from 16.3% to 8.2%, and cuts MMLU accuracy regressions by 57% (1071 to 465). On Gemma2-9B, mild smoothing keeps HiFA4 within 0.7 pp of BF16 while reducing MMLU regressions by 27%. On LLaMA3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4B, where Smooth-QK is disabled, P-Reordering with the adopted Q-Mean auxiliary still reduces full-set MMLU regressions by 41-52%. A preliminary instruction-scheduling analysis projects a 35.4% critical-path latency reduction relative to BF16 by fusing the softmax normalizer into the PV Cube GEMM; on-hardware validation is left to future work.

</details>


### [89] [Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation with Prefix Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04763)
*Baohao Liao,Hanze Dong,Christof Monz,Xinxing Xu,Li Dong,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReOPD（重放前缀在线策略蒸馏）方法，通过重用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，在不进行实际环境交互的情况下实现多轮智能体任务的在线策略蒸馏，解决了在线策略蒸馏成本高和"前缀陷阱"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全在线的策略蒸馏成本极高，因为每次更新都需要学生模型重新与环境交互，并且需要在访问的历史状态上查询教师模型。作者希望降低这一成本，使蒸馏过程更高效可扩展。

Method: ReOPD将预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，学生仅在选定步骤上执行动作，教师提供密集的逐步骤监督但不执行新的环境交互。通过步长衰减采样策略，优先选择早期、分布偏移较小的前缀，平衡学生占据与教师可靠性之间的分布偏移。

Result: 在数学推理（Python环境）和搜索环境等多个任务上，使用不同规模的教师和学生模型，ReOPD保持或提升了在线策略蒸馏的准确性，学生训练期间零工具调用，每训练步至少快4倍。

Conclusion: ReOPD将昂贵的智能体-环境交互转化为可重用的离线资源，实现了跨工具、任务和环境的可扩展蒸馏。

Abstract: We study on-policy distillation (OPD) for agentic tasks, where an LLM agent interacts with an environment over multiple turns and a student imitates a teacher over these multi-turn interaction histories. Fully online OPD is costly because each update requires fresh student rollouts through the environment and teacher queries at visited histories. We propose Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation (ReOPD), an off-environment alternative that reuses pre-collected teacher trajectories as replayed prefixes: the student acts at selected steps, while the teacher provides dense per-step supervision without executing new environment interactions. We show that multi-turn OPD introduces a prefix trap: making histories more student-on-policy improves relevance to the student, but can query the teacher on histories where its target is unreliable. This creates a two-sided distribution shift between student occupancy and teacher reliability. ReOPD addresses this by treating multi-turn OPD as a reliability-aware prefix distribution design and implements it with a simple step-decaying sampling schedule that emphasizes early, lower-shift prefixes. Across mathematical reasoning with Python and search environments over multiple teacher and student model scales, ReOPD preserves or improves OPD-level accuracy, uses zero tool calls during student training, and is at least 4$\times$ faster per training step than OPD. ReOPD therefore turns expensive agent-environment interaction into a reusable offline resource, enabling scalable distillation across tools, tasks, and environments.

</details>


### [90] [Quantize the Target, Quantize the Drafter: Efficient Inference with Qwen3.5-4B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04244)
*Jaeyeon Kim,Jewon Lee,Bo-Kyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合量化感知蒸馏与分块扩散草稿模型的推测解码方案，在NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟推理，相比基线获得6.978倍平均加速，排名竞赛第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟服务，满足竞赛要求的同时保证模型质量。

Method: 量化感知蒸馏保持量化精度+两阶段训练的分块扩散草稿模型（先学高精度再适配低精度）+推测解码+草稿模型自身的量化与滑动窗口注意力优化。

Result: 在满足质量阈值的前提下，平均加速6.978倍，综合排名第三。

Conclusion: 所提方法在推理效率和模型质量之间取得了良好平衡，为实际LLM推理提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: This report describes our approach to the Efficient Qwen Competition, where the goal is to enable low-latency serving of Qwen3.5-4B on a resource-constrained NVIDIA A10G GPU. Our system combines a quantized target model with speculative decoding. To recover accuracy, we apply quantization-aware distillation to the target model while retaining the original quantization grid. To speed up decoding, a block-diffusion drafter specialized for the quantized target model is trained using a two-stage procedure: first learning from the high-precision target and then adapting to the low-precision target. Because the drafter is invoked at every speculative decoding step, we further reduce its overhead with quantization and sliding-window attention, preserving draft-token acceptance while improving long-context decoding latency. As a result, our submission achieves a 6.978$\times$ average speedup over the baseline while satisfying the required quality thresholds, ranking 3rd overall. We hope these results provide useful insights for practical LLM inference. The code and resources are available at https://github.com/nota-github/adaptfm-quant-dflash

</details>


### [91] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394)
*Shiyuan Feng,Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Hanlin Wu,Zhilong Zhang,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct-OPD方法，通过将弱模型RL前后的策略差异作为隐式奖励信号蒸馏给强模型，实现弱到强的推理能力迁移，避免在强模型上重复昂贵的RL训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽然能提升语言模型推理能力，但在每个新强模型上重复RL训练成本极高（需要大量rollout），随着模型规模增大，后训练本身成为瓶颈。因此需要一种更高效的方法复用已有RL成果。

Method: Direct-OPD：在小模型上运行RL后，将RL前后的策略差异（log-ratio）作为密集隐式奖励信号，在强模型自身on-policy状态上应用该信号进行蒸馏，而不是直接模仿RL后的弱教师模型。

Result: Qwen3-1.7B在AIME 2024上从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅需8张A100 GPU训练4小时；优于直接RL和直接蒸馏方法；支持多个策略偏移的顺序组合。

Conclusion: RL的训练成果可以作为隐式奖励信号跨模型规模复用，而不仅仅是作为最终模型被模仿，这为高效的大模型后训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.

</details>


### [92] [NKI-Agent: Domain-Specific Fine-Tuning and Agentic Tool Use for Neuron Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04395)
*Junjie Tang,Jun Huan,Hao Zhou,Yuhao Zhang,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NKI-Agent，首个结合领域监督微调（SFT）与编译-验证-修复智能体循环的 NKI 内核生成系统，针对 AWS Trainium/Inferentia 等新兴 AI 加速器，在真实 Trn1 硬件上达到 77.3% 的通过率，并对比了多种模型与训练策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的内核生成方法在 CUDA 上表现优异，但针对 AWS Trainium/Inferentia 等新兴 AI 加速器的 NKI 内核自动生成与优化尚未被充分研究。NKI 编程面临多引擎架构、tile 编程、显式数据移动等多重挑战，且缺乏公开训练数据、基准测试和工具增强智能体。

Method: 将 CUDA-Agent 框架适配到 Neuron 硬件，构建 NKI-Agent 系统，包含：（1）6,000 个 NKI 内核生成任务进行 SFT 训练；（2）编译-验证-修复智能体循环；（3）250 任务的 NKIBench 基准测试（三个难度级别）；（4）对比多种模型（Claude Opus 4.8、Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B 等）和训练策略（SFT、GRPO）。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8 配合 rank-aware system prompt 在 150 任务 NKIBench 上达到 77.3% 通过率；无工具时 Opus 4.8 单次生成仅 6%；SFT 微调的 Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B 在 60 任务子集上以 1/100 成本达到 25.0%，优于 Claude Sonnet 4（15.0%）；GRPO 方法未超越 SFT。

Conclusion: 工具增强智能体对 NKI 内核生成至关重要；SFT 能有效提升小模型性价比；GRPO 在二元编译奖励下效果有限，为基于 RL 的内核生成奖励设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Recent agentic approaches to LLM-based kernel generation have achieved impressive results on CUDA. For emerging AI accelerators such as AWS Trainium and Inferentia, automated kernel generation and optimization remain largely unaddressed. Writing kernels for these chips via the Neuron Kernel Interface (NKI) is particularly challenging: developers must navigate a multi-engine architecture, tile-based programming, and explicit data movement across multi-level memory hierarchy. Moreover, no publicly-available training data, benchmarks, or tool-augmented agents exist for this domain. We introduce NKI-Agent, the first system combining domain-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with a compile-verify-fix agent loop for NKI kernel generation. We adapt the existing CUDA-Agent framework to Neuron hardware, curate 6,000 NKI kernel generation tasks for training, and construct NKIBench, a 250-task benchmark across three difficulty levels. Evaluated on real Trn1 hardware, NKI-Agent with Claude Opus 4.8 and a rank-aware system prompt achieves a 77.3% pass rate on the 150-task NKIBench. We show that tool use is critical: Opus 4.8 scores 6% in single-shot mode without agent tools. On a 60-task subset, we show that an SFT-trained Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B achieves 25.0% pass rate at 1/100th the cost, outperforming Claude Sonnet 4 (15.0%). We also report that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with binary compilation reward fails to improve over SFT, providing guidance on reward design for RL-based kernel generation.

</details>


### [93] [Full-Stack FP4: Stable LLM Pretraining with Quantized Projections, Optimizers, and Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04422)
*Siyu Ding,Mingchuan Ma,Jiabo Tong,Xingrun Xing,Ziming Wang,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Full-Stack FP4，首个完整的 NVFP4 预训练框架，通过模块级精度策略解决线性层、优化器状态和注意力机制在4比特流水线中的数值稳定性瓶颈，在3B/64B-token预训练中实现接近BF16的性能（损失差距仅1.47%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NVFP4预训练方法主要针对Transformer线性层，忽略了优化器状态、优化器算术和注意力机制的4比特支持，导致无法实现稳定的全栈4比特预训练，这三个核心模块存在独特的数值失效模式。

Method: 提出模块级精度策略：1) 线性层采用LoRA-SVD轻量级分解抑制量化噪声；2) 优化器设计AdamW二阶矩变换实现NVFP4存储，支持原生NVFP4 Newton-Schulz迭代；3) 注意力机制采用混合精度方案，量化Q/K/V和反向dS，保护脆弱路径为BF16，配合统一张量复用维持前后向对齐。

Result: 线性层损失差距从1.40%降至0.61%；3B/64B-token预训练整体损失差距仅1.47%，验证了接近BF16性能的稳定端到端NVFP4 LLM预训练。

Conclusion: 所有模块即插即用，累计提升稳定性和效率，验证了可行且稳定的端到端NVFP4大模型预训练方案。

Abstract: Recent NVFP4 pretraining methods mainly target transformer linear layers, leaving optimizer states, optimizer arithmetic and attention underexplored in 4-bit pipelines. This critical gap blocks stable full-stack 4-bit pretraining, as the three core modules exhibit unique numerical failure patterns: linear layers hit hard quantization noise limits with dimension-propagated error amplification; AdamW second moments are heavy-tailed non-negative values fragile to low-precision denominators; attention carries error-prone computation paths demanding strict forward-backward quantization consistency. We propose Full-Stack FP4, the first complete NVFP4 pretraining framework resolving all three stability bottlenecks via module-wise precision strategies. For linear projections, LoRA-SVD lightweight decomposition suppresses quantization noise and breaks the direct-quantization error ceiling, shrinking the linear-only loss gap from 1.40% to 0.61%. For optimizers, we design AdamW second-moment transformation for robust NVFP4 storage and fully support native NVFP4 Newton-Schulz iterations for the Root (Muon) optimizer. For attention, a mixed-precision scheme quantizes Q/K/V and backward dS while guarding vulnerable paths in BF16, paired with unified tensor reuse to sustain forward-backward alignment. We further analyze fast error accumulation in naive low-bit matrix multiplication and the extreme sensitivity of PV / dOV^T attention branches. All modules are plug-and-play with cumulative stability and efficiency improvements. Our 3B/64B-token pretraining validates near-BF16 performance with merely 1.47% loss gap, verifying feasible stable end-to-end NVFP4 LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [94] [Layer-Parallel Inference Reduces Encrypted Nonlinear Depth in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04819)
*Ligong Han,Kai Xu,Hao Wang,Ruijiang Gao,Akash Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化牛顿层并行性（SNLP）能否使Transformer推理在FHE下更高效，通过减少层间非线性顺序深度来降低引导操作次数，实验表明SNLP将引导次数从53降至20（2.65x），困惑度仅增加1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE支持加密数据计算，但实际应用的Transformer推理受限于大量非线性块的顺序组合，导致性能瓶颈，因此需要更FHE友好的架构。

Method: 提出SNLP方法，将每层Transformer的非线性顺序深度从L层减少为少量求解器迭代加线性结构化校正，并基于切比雪夫多项式近似的仿真框架评估误差累积。

Result: 在0.5B模型上，SNLP将符号引导从53降至20（2.65x），困惑度仅增加1.2%，错误放大从1.42x降至1.36x；在所有测试模型中SNLP的误差放大均低于顺序推理。

Conclusion: SNLP与块级FHE友好的算子设计互为补充而非替代，softmax近似主导误差预算，CKKS算术噪声影响可忽略。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, but practical encrypted Transformer inference is bottlenecked by the sequential composition of many nonlinear blocks. We study whether Structured Newton Layer Parallelism (SNLP) can make this inter-layer composition more FHE-friendly: each Transformer block still requires polynomial approximations for operations such as softmax and RMSNorm, but SNLP reduces the layerwise sequential nonlinear depth from L stages to a small number of solver iterations plus linear structured corrections. Using a simulation framework based on Chebyshev polynomial approximations, we measure error accumulation under sequential versus SNLP inference across 8 models and 4 architecture families. On a 0.5B IDN-trained model, SNLP reduces symbolic bootstraps from 53 to 20 (2.65x) with only +1.2% perplexity degradation, while lowering error amplification (1.36x vs. 1.42x). Across all tested models, SNLP has lower amplification than sequential inference. Ablations show that softmax approximation dominates the error budget and CKKS arithmetic noise is negligible in our setting, suggesting that SNLP is complementary to block-level FHE-friendly operator design rather than a replacement for it.

</details>


### [95] [When Do Foundation Models Pay Off? A Break-Even Analysis of Pretrained Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04919)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Frank Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了时序基础模型（Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）相对于经典方法（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost）的盈亏平衡点。在30个基准数据集上、六种训练集规模下对比，发现15个数据集上基础模型无条件胜出，6个数据集上经典方法仅需2%数据即胜出，其余9个存在盈亏平衡阈值。提出了一个两步决策框架，帮助从业者在投入GPU基础设施前判断是否值得部署基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时序基础模型需要GPU基础设施和工程开销，且不确定是否比XGBoost更好，现有研究缺乏系统的盈亏平衡分析来指导这一投资决策。

Method: 在30个基准数据集上，对比零样本和LoRA微调的基础模型（Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）与经典基线方法（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost），在训练集规模从2%到100%共六档下进行系统比较，并基于数据集长度和季节性强弱提出两步决策框架。

Result: 15/30数据集上基础模型无条件胜出；6/30数据集上经典方法仅需2%数据即超越零样本基础模型；9/30数据集存在24-8361样本的盈亏平衡阈值。当n_train<700且季节性不可忽略时，零样本基础模型是最优选择。

Conclusion: 提出了一个实用的两步决策框架，帮助从业者在投入全部基础设施前判断是否部署基础模型。LoRA微调在短序列上可能降低性能，四类数据集特征可解释剩余案例的机制差异。

Abstract: Deploying a time series foundation model requires GPU infrastructure, engineering overhead, and carries no guarantee of improvement over XGBoost. We provide the first systematic break-even analysis answering when this investment pays off. Across 30 benchmark datasets, we compare zero-shot and LoRA fine-tuned foundation models (Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama) against classical baselines (Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost) at six training set sizes from 2% to 100% of available data. Foundation models outperform classical methods at every evaluated training fraction on 15 of 30 datasets -- GPU deployment is unconditionally justified on these regardless of data volume. On 6 datasets, classical methods surpass zero-shot foundation models with as little as 2% of training data (21-2,768 samples); on the remaining 9, break-even ranges from 24 to 8,361 samples. One robust deployment rule requires no model training: if n_train < 700 and seasonality is non-negligible, use FM zero-shot and skip fine-tuning -- this resolves 10 of 30 deployment decisions immediately. Contrary to common practice, LoRA fine-tuning can actively degrade performance on short series. We operationalise these findings as a two-step decision framework -- compute dataset length and seasonality strength, run a brief 5-10% pilot only if needed -- enabling practitioners to make the FM-versus-classical decision before committing to full infrastructure. Four dataset features motivate mechanistic hypotheses for the remaining cases, though reliable automated prediction at this benchmark scale remains an open problem. Code, benchmark, and decision tools are available at https://github.com/nicolaisi/fm-breakeven.

</details>


### [96] [The Map Behind the Flow: Finite-Step Gradient Descent as a Dynamical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04993)
*Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定步长梯度下降作为离散动力系统的性质，通过一系列可精确求解的模型（深度线性链、因子化模型、宽线性网络等）揭示了有限步长导致的边缘稳定性、振荡、平衡等动力学现象，证明学习率不仅是数值稳定参数，更是决定训练动力学吸引子和表示选择的结枃参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的许多现象（边缘稳定性、尖锐度振荡、弹射阶段、平衡、向更平坦表示移动等）本质上是动力学的，但被小步长梯度流极限所忽略。现有方法无法很好地捕捉训练映射本身的这些效应。

Method: 将固定步长梯度下降建模为离散动力系统，构建一系列可精确求解的模型层次：从深度线性链的标量约化（四次损失、三次梯度映射），到大深度缩放下的通用Ricker型映射，再嵌入回因子化模型、宽线性网络（谱边梯子）、数据耦合、非线性激活和随机目标等场景。

Result: 边缘稳定性不是优化的崩溃，而是训练映射的第一次分岔；有限步长打破了梯度流的守恒律，导致因子化不平衡收缩和残差振荡；宽线性网络产生谱边梯子，最优学习率可以位于第一个谱边之后；有限步长振荡驱动对齐、平衡和表示选择。

Conclusion: 学习率不仅是数值稳定性参数，更是训练动力学的结构参数，决定了梯度下降的吸引子并塑造了其选择的表示。

Abstract: Many phenomena of deep learning are dynamical: they concern not only which minima exist, but how gradient descent reaches, avoids, or selects among them. Edge-of-stability behavior, sharpness oscillations, catapult phases, balancing, and movement toward flatter representations are effects of the training map itself, and are poorly captured by the small-step gradient-flow limit.
  This paper studies fixed-step gradient descent as a discrete dynamical system in a hierarchy of exactly solvable models retaining basic structures of deep learning: depth, factorization, width, data coupling, activation, and stochasticity. The starting point is the balanced scalar reduction of a deep linear chain, giving a quartic loss and a cubic gradient map whose post-edge behavior is explicit. Under the natural large-depth scaling, this dynamics converges to a universal Ricker-type map. The edge of stability is therefore not a breakdown of optimization, but the first bifurcation of the training map.
  Embedding the scalar dynamics back into factored models turns these regimes into learning phenomena. Finite steps break conservation laws of gradient flow and contract factorization imbalance; residual oscillations move parameters toward flatter, more balanced representations. Wider linear networks produce a ladder of spectral edges, so the optimal learning rate can lie beyond the first edge. Data coupling, nonlinear activations, and stochastic targets preserve the same organizing principle: finite-step oscillations drive alignment, balancing, and representation selection. Thus the learning rate is not merely a numerical stability parameter. It is a structural parameter of the training dynamics, determining its attractors and shaping the representations gradient descent selects.

</details>


### [97] [CollabEval: Statistically Efficient Collaborative Model Evaluation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05046)
*Adam Fisch,Daniel Deutsch,Joshua Maynez,Alekh Agarwal,Jonathan Berant,William Cohen,Amir Globerson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CollabEval方法，利用历史模型评估运行之间的依赖关系，通过矩阵补全和预测驱动推断技术，在少量标注预算下无偏估计模型评估指标均值并给出有效置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型评估在模型开发过程中重复进行且资源消耗大，现有方法统计效率低，需要更高效的评估方法。

Method: 将模型评估视为M×N评分矩阵的补全问题（M为模型数，N为评估提示数），对目标模型仅标注p比例的提示，利用预测驱动推断构建低秩近似矩阵，将重构值作为控制变量，实现无偏估计和有效置信区间。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和稀疏度p下，CollabEval相比基线方法显著降低了置信区间大小和点估计的均方误差。

Conclusion: 利用历史运行依赖关系可以显著提升模型评估的统计效率，是一种简单、有效且有原则的方法。

Abstract: Evaluating generative AI models is a routine, but resource-intensive, process that is conducted over and over again during the course of model development. In this work, we propose Collaborative Evaluation (CollabEval), a simple, effective, and principled method for exploiting dependencies between historical runs of different models on the same tasks to improve statistical efficiency. Specifically, our approach treats model evaluation as a matrix completion problem over an $M \times N$ matrix of evaluation scores, where $M$ is the total number of models and $N$ is the total number of evaluation prompts. We assume that a subset of these $M$ models are targeted for evaluation. For these target models only a small fraction, $p$, of prompts has been annotated with evaluation scores. Leveraging recent results in prediction-powered inference, we build a low-rank approximation of the score matrix, and use the reconstructed values as control variates in a manner that guarantees unbiased estimates of the true evaluation metric mean, in addition to statistically valid confidence intervals. Empirically, across a wide range of datasets, models, and sparsity levels $p$, we find that CollabEval substantially reduces the mean confidence interval size, and the mean squared error of the point estimate, compared to baseline methods at the same annotation budget.

</details>


### [98] [KVpop -- Key-Value Cache Compression with Predictive Online Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05061)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Thomas Schmied,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVpop提出了一种通过学习未来注意力信号来指导KV缓存驱逐策略的方法，在固定预算下通过直接监督保留/丢弃决策来降低KV缓存增长带来的内存和带宽开销，在数学推理任务上实现了高压缩率下接近全注意力性能的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码中KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，成为主要瓶颈；现有基于静态启发式或代理分数的驱逐方法难以准确预测未来token的效用，导致驱逐决策脆弱。

Method: KVpop学习一个固定预算的KV驱逐策略，通过直接监督保留/丢弃决策进行训练。使用新颖的未来注意力目标训练评分器，无需具体化密集注意力图即可高效计算；引入基于延迟记忆的评分器，推迟若干步评分以利用近期上下文信息。

Result: 在AIME和HMMT数学推理任务上，KVpop在Qwen3-4B上以75% KV缓存压缩率保留了98%的全注意力性能，88%压缩率保留97%；在Qwen3-8B上表现更强，接近全教师性能。

Conclusion: 使用未来注意力信号监督KV驱逐可以在保持生成质量的同时显著降低内存成本。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache growth is a major bottleneck in autoregressive decoding, as memory and bandwidth scale linearly with context length. Existing KV eviction methods often rely on static heuristics or proxy scores, which poorly track future token utility and cause brittle eviction as relevance shifts. To address this, we introduce KVpop, which learns a fixed-budget KV eviction policy by directly supervising the keep-or-drop decision. The scorer is trained against a novel future-attention target, computed efficiently without materializing dense attention maps. We further introduce a delayed memory-based scorer that, uniquely among learned eviction methods, defers scoring for a fixed number of steps to exploit near-future context. On AIME and HMMT mathematical reasoning, KVpop retains 98% of full-attention performance on Qwen3-4B at 75% KV cache compression and 97% at 88% compression, consistently outperforming established eviction baselines. Qwen3-8B shows even stronger results, reaching near-full teacher performance. These results show that supervising eviction with future-attention signals cuts memory costs while maintaining quality.

</details>


### [99] [FAST: A Holistic Framework for Optimizing Memory-I/O, Computation, and Sampling in Temporal GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05095)
*Yushu Cai,Qingrui Zhu,Lei Liu,Kai Sheng,Hao Chen,Xin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAST是一个端到端加速时序图神经网络（TGNN）训练的系统框架，通过联合优化采样、内存I/O和计算，引入SlimCache（批内压缩+跨批缓存）、线程高效图算子和拓扑感知采样策略，在真实大规模动态图上实现平均2.1倍（最高4.7倍）的加速且不损失模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TGNN训练系统在内存I/O、不规则计算和时序邻居采样这三个瓶颈上孤立优化，导致在规模扩展时性能不佳，缺乏端到端的整体优化方案。

Method: 提出FAST框架：(1) SlimCache利用批内压缩和跨批缓存减少主机-设备数据传输；(2) 设计面向稀疏时序子图的线程高效图算子，提升GPU缓存局部性；(3) 采用拓扑感知采样策略提升CPU缓存局部性，加速时序邻居采样。

Result: 在真实大规模动态图上，相比现有最优系统实现平均2.1倍（最高4.7倍）的端到端训练加速，且不牺牲模型精度。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化采样、内存I/O和计算三个瓶颈，FAST有效提升了TGNN训练的可扩展性，为大规模动态图学习提供了高效的系统解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) are widely used for learning from dynamic graphs in applications such as recommendation, social network analysis, and traffic forecasting. However, scaling TGNN training to large dynamic graphs remains challenging due to three intertwined bottlenecks: memory I/O, irregular computation, and temporal neighbor sampling. Existing systems often optimize these stages in isolation, leaving substantial performance headroom on the table. We present FAST, a holistic framework that accelerates end-to-end TGNN training by jointly optimizing sampling, memory I/O, and computation. FAST introduces SlimCache, which exploits within-batch compression and cross-batch caching to reduce host-device data movement under limited GPU memory budgets. It further designs thread-efficient graph operators tailored to sparse temporal subgraphs, improving GPU cache locality and reducing the latency of aggregation and edge softmax. In addition, FAST employs a topology-aware sampling strategy that improves CPU cache locality and accelerates temporal neighbor sampling. Extensive experiments on real-world large dynamic graphs show that FAST achieves an average of 2.1x (up to 4.7x) speedup over state-of-the-art systems without sacrificing model accuracy.

</details>


### [100] [PDEFlow: Autonomous Agentic PDE Pipelines for Neural Operator Learning and Solver-Free Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05134)
*Akshat Jani,Prathamesh Gadekar,Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDEFlow是一个自治智能体框架，将用户层面的ODE/PDE描述转化为求解器支持的神经算子流水线，涵盖问题规范、数据生成、算子训练和推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少在大量相关物理配置的指定、模拟、学习和查询中的手动干预，实现可重复的科学与工程工作流自动化。

Method: 构建状态化输入图处理多轮自然语言输入，使用FEniCSx有限元后端求解方程生成数据，通过注册表接口支持多种神经算子（当前实例化为多分支贝叶斯DeepONet）的训练与部署。

Result: 在基准ODE/PDE任务上，PDEFlow能够构建有效规范、生成求解器支持的数据集、训练稳态和瞬态问题的神经算子，并从检查点提供无求解器的预测。

Conclusion: PDEFlow为需要重复指定、模拟、学习和查询物理配置的科学与工程工作流提供了减少手动干预的自动化框架。

Abstract: We present PDEFlow, an autonomous agentic framework that turns user-level ODE and PDE descriptions into solver-backed neural-operator pipelines. The workflow links problem specification, data generation, operator training, and checkpoint-based inference. A stateful input graph converts multi-turn natural-language input and user edits into validated problem specifications. The data-generation module then samples parameters, solves the configured governing-equation with FEniCSx finite-element backend, and stores the solutions as operator-ready tensors. The training and inference stages use a registry-based interface, allowing different neural operators to be trained and deployed without changing the surrounding pipeline. In the current implementation, we instantiate this interface with a multi-branch Bayesian DeepONet. Experiments on benchmark ODE and PDE tasks show that PDEFlow can construct valid specifications, generate solver-backed datasets, train neural operators across steady and transient problem classes, and provide solver-free predictions from saved checkpoints. The framework is designed for repeatable scientific and engineering workflows where many related physics configurations must be specified, simulated, learned, and queried with minimal manual intervention.

</details>


### [101] [Advances in Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05280)
*Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Log-NCDE、Linear NCDE和Structured Linear NCDE三种方法，将神经受控微分方程（NCDE）的训练速度提升最多三个数量级，同时保持或提升其在时间序列基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCDE虽然具有最大表达能力和连续时间灵活性，但其前向传播计算昂贵且本质上是顺序的，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。本文旨在提升NCDE的训练效率和可扩展性。

Method: 1) Log-NCDE：利用Log-ODE方法高效近似NCDE的解，提升计算速度和经验性能；2) Linear NCDE：用线性向量场替代非线性向量场，实现闭式解和并行计算，且不牺牲理论表达能力；3) Structured Linear NCDE：使用结构化线性向量场进一步提高效率，同时保持理论表达能力和经验性能。

Result: 将NCDE每个训练步骤的时间减少最多三个数量级，并在多个时间序列基准上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过近似求解、线性化和结构化线性化三种互补策略，显著提升了NCDE的训练效率和可扩展性，使其在保持理论表达能力的同时具备实际可用性。

Abstract: Many real-world systems evolve continuously, yet most machine learning models interpret time series as discrete sequences. Continuous-time approaches instead treat time series as samples from an underlying input path, a formulation that naturally accommodates irregularly sampled or oversampled data. Among these, Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) are a maximally expressive class of models that parametrise a vector field using a neural network and evolve their hidden state by solving a dynamical system driven by the input path. NCDEs typically use a non-linear vector field, so their expressive power and continuous-time flexibility come at the cost of a forward pass that is both computationally expensive and inherently sequential, limiting their scalability and practical applicability.
  This thesis advances the training and scalability of NCDEs through three complementary contributions. First, building on neural rough differential equations, Log-NCDEs apply the Log-ODE method to efficiently approximate an NCDE's solution during training, improving both computational speed and empirical performance. Second, Linear NCDEs replace the non-linear vector field with a linear one, enabling closed-form solutions and parallel-in-time computation without sacrificing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs use structured linear vector fields to further enhance efficiency while maintaining theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance.
  Collectively, these methods reduce the time per training step for an NCDE by up to three orders of magnitude while achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse time series benchmarks.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Only What Valid Adapters Can Express: Subspace-Constrained Adaptation Against Fine-Tuning Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05300)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在可信任务适配器池估计的低维子空间内进行参数约束的微调方法，相比全参数LoRA,该方法在保持任务性能的同时，能有效防御标签反转和自适应后门攻击，但牺牲了池覆盖不佳任务上的峰值塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调仍留有大量空间让中毒目标被表示和优化，需要更安全的适配方案。

Method: 从196个公开LoRA适配器中估计共享低维子空间，将梯度更新约束在该子空间的128个坐标上。

Result: 子空间内30-38%权重范数冗余；约束学习在干净数据上匹配全LoRA，在标签反转下保持62-96%准确率而LoRA降至3-26%；约束学习无法拟合腐败数据，损失差达120倍；自适应后门攻击在目标行为不在池中时成功率8%（LoRA为100%），目标与池行为一致时85%。

Conclusion: 子空间约束微调以牺牲峰值塑性换取安全属性，在池覆盖好的任务上有效防御中毒攻击，但依赖池本身可信，且在池覆盖差的任务上不如无约束微调。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning still leaves a broad space of behavior-changing updates reachable, so a poisoned objective can be represented and optimized. We study an alternative: adaptation constrained to the subspace estimated from a trusted pool of existing task adapters. On flan-t5-large with 196 public LoRA adapters, we show that (1) the functionally relevant content of an adapter lies in a low-dimensional shared subspace, 30 to 38 percent of its weight norm being redundant under the evaluated task distributions; (2) gradient adaptation restricted to 128 coordinates on this subspace matches full LoRA fine-tuning on clean classification data, while under targeted label inversion LoRA collapses to 3-26 percent exact match and the constrained learner keeps 62-96 percent on the tasks the pool covers; (3) the constrained learner cannot fit corrupted data, its adaptation loss separating clean from garbage by two orders of magnitude (120x), an out-of-distribution signal without an extra detector; and (4) against an adaptive backdoor attacker who optimizes within the subspace, the attack is blocked (8 percent success versus 100 for LoRA) on the task where its target behavior is unlike anything in the pool, and only partially blocked (85 percent) when the target coincides with a common pool behavior. On these two tasks the outcome is consistent with how close the target is to the pool's directions, which suggests but does not establish a pool-relative boundary. The mechanism trades peak plasticity for these properties: on tasks the pool covers poorly, unconstrained fine-tuning wins, and the protection assumes the pool itself is trusted. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [103] [CompactionRL: Reinforcement Learning with Context Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05378)
*Yujiang Li,Zhenyu Hou,Yi Jing,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CompactionRL，一种结合上下文压缩的强化学习策略，用于训练长周期LLM智能体，通过联合优化任务执行和摘要生成，解决有限上下文窗口问题，在SWE-bench等编码任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期LLM智能体在执行复杂任务时，交互轨迹可能超过模型的最大上下文长度，导致任务无法完成。现有上下文压缩方法虽能通过摘要化压缩历史状态，但如何将压缩机制融入强化学习训练尚未被充分探索。

Method: CompactionRL框架联合优化任务执行和摘要生成，采用token级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计（GAE），使LLM智能体能从压缩后的长周期轨迹中学习。

Result: 在GLM-4.5-Air模型上，SWE-bench Verified Pass@1达66.8%（+7.0），Terminal-Bench 2.0达24.5%（+3.1）；在GLM-4.7-Flash模型上分别提升5.5和6.8个百分点。该方法已部署在GLM-5.2模型的RL训练管线中。

Conclusion: CompactionRL是一种有效的强化学习策略，能够训练长周期LLM智能体在有限上下文窗口下完成任务，实验证明在编码任务上具有一致的性能提升，并已用于大规模模型训练。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic LLMs are increasingly limited by finite context windows, as extended interaction trajectories can exceed the maximum context length before a task is completed. Context compaction offers a natural solution by summarizing previous interaction states and continuing the rollout under a compressed context, but incorporating compaction into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. We propose CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning strategy to train long-horizon agentic LLMs with context compaction. Our approach jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation with token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation. This design enables the LLM agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. We train CompactionRL on top of open models and observe consistent performance gains on agentic coding tasks. CompactionRL enables the open GLM-4.5-Air model (106B-A30B) to achieve Pass@1 scores of 66.8% on SWE-bench Verified and 24.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, with absolute gains of 7.0 and 3.1 points, respectively. Built upon GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B), CompactionRL improves Pass@1 by 5.5 and 6.8 points, reaching 56.0% on SWE-bench Verified and 20.2% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, respectively. CompactionRL is thus deployed in the RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02542)
*Yuan Zhang,Jingfei Ni,Guanchen Lu,Shiqi Zhang,Qingshan Xu,Chi Liu,Xin Nie,Wenjie Xu,Lin Gao,Zhiyuan Cheng,Mingxin Zhou,Jiajia Wu,Diyuan Liu,Jia Pan,Chao Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni，一个统一的多模态具身基础模型，通过共享多模态自注意力机制联合建模视觉、语言和动作，采用"大脑-小脑"协作架构（VLM+VGM作为大脑，AGM作为小脑），并在多阶段训练策略下使用包含人类演示和机器人交互的综合数据集进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体通常在视觉-语言推理、视频世界建模和动作生成上各自独立建模，级联流水线会产生接口瓶颈和累积预测误差，缺乏一个统一框架来联合建模这三种模态。

Method: 提出统一多模态基础模型，包含VLM、VGM和AGM三个组件，通过共享多模态自注意力实现通信；采用"大脑-小脑"协作架构；构建包含动作标注/无标注的具身视频、推理、感知和通用图文数据的综合数据集；采用四阶段渐进训练策略。

Result: 论文仅展示方法设计，未提供具体实验结果数据。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态框架相比级联流水线能更好地处理长程具身任务，共享注意力机制和四阶段训练策略有助于模型联合学习视觉-语言推理、视频预测和动作生成。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied agents must understand multimodal instructions, anticipate how their environment will evolve, and produce precise control actions over extended horizons. Existing approaches typically specialize in visual-language reasoning, video-based world modeling, or action generation, while cascaded pipelines that first synthesize future observations and then infer actions can introduce interface bottlenecks and compound prediction errors. We present iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni, a unified multimodal foundation model that jointly models vision(videos and images), language, and action within a single Omni framework. Its modality-specific visual-language, video-generation, and action-generation components communicate through shared multimodal self-attention. This design establishes brain-cerebellum collaboration: the vision-language modeland video generation model form a high-level brain for instruction understanding, task planning, progress tracking, and future visual-state prediction, whereas the action generation modelserves as a low-level cerebellum that directly converts planned subgoals and shared multimodal context into executable action chunks. To develop these capabilities, we combine action-annotated and action-free embodied videos from human demonstrations and robot interactions with embodied reasoning, embodied perception, and general-purpose image-text data to construct a comprehensive dataset. We further adopt a four-stage strategy that progressively trains the VLM, VGM, and AGM before jointly fine-tuning the complete model.

</details>


### [105] [ASK in the Dark: Uncertainty-Gated LLM Assistance under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02686)
*Juarez Monteiro,Nathan Gavenski,Guilherme Lima,Francisco Galuppo,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Adriano Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ASK+方法，通过向小语言模型提供轨迹感知上下文（部分地图、已访问位置、动作历史）和结构化思维链推理，解决部分可观察环境下强化学习智能体难以有效利用SLM引导的问题，在多个环境上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观察环境下的强化学习智能体信息不完全，天然适合利用小语言模型（SLM）的广泛推理先验进行引导，但现有不确定性门控方法中SLM几乎从不贡献独立动作（覆盖率为零），原因在于裸自我中心提示缺乏足够上下文，这是一个上下文问题而非能力问题。

Method: 提出ASK+，向SLM提供轨迹感知上下文（部分公开地图、已访问位置、动作历史）和结构化思维链推理，将SLM从被动冗余检查转变为更具信息量的顾问，偶尔纠正策略；同时论证预测熵信号度量的是动作不确定性而非状态不确定性，在POMDP中仍然有效。

Result: 在DoorKey上从89%提升至93%；FourRooms从53%提升至70%；HigherLower达到73.7%，匹配SLM上限。Qwen3.5-2B表现匹配或超越Qwen3.5-4B，提示设计和选择性门控的收益远超模型规模增长。

Conclusion: 提示设计和选择性门控主导了模型规模的影响，无需大模型即可实现有效的SLM引导；预测熵在POMDP中作为查询信号仍然有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents operating under partial observability must act on incomplete information, making them natural candidates for guidance from small language models (SLMs) that carry broad reasoning priors. Yet integrating SLM guidance into this setting has proven difficult: across all test environments, vanilla uncertainty-gated approaches achieve an overwrite rate at or near zero, meaning the SLM almost never contributes an independent action. We trace this failure to the bare egocentric prompt, which provides insufficient context for genuine reasoning, and identify it as a context problem rather than a capacity problem. We propose ASK+, which supplies the SLM with trajectory-aware context (a partially revealed map, visited positions, and action history) and structured chain-of-thought reasoning, converting it from a passive redundancy check into a more informative consultant that occasionally corrects the policy. We further establish that the predictive entropy signal used for selective querying measures action uncertainty rather than state uncertainty and remains informative in POMDPs, making uncertainty-gated assistance viable beyond fully observable settings. The stateful prompt drives substantial gains: on DoorKey, where vanilla ASK matches PPO (both 89%), ASK+ reaches 93% success; on FourRooms, success climbs from 53% to 70%; on HigherLower, accuracy reaches 73.7%, matching the SLM-only upper bound. Across all environments, Qwen3.5-2B matches or exceeds Qwen3.5-4B, confirming that prompt design and selective gating dominate the impact of model scale, enabling guidance without large models.

</details>
